      Jupiter stands in a kendra from the Moon. The mind is backed by wisdom: reputation, counsel-worthiness and resilience in public life, strongest when Jupiter is dignified.

      Mars (an angle lord) and Jupiter (a trine lord) stand in mutual aspect. The classical engine of rank: the union of dharma and karma lords lifts status, and its results ripen in the dasha periods of the two planets involved.

  [dhana-5-2] Dhana Yoga (5th–2nd lords)  planets=Su,Ve
      The 5th lord Sun joins the 2nd lord Venus — fortune's lord touching the wealth axis. Money arrives through merit, timing or backing rather than grind alone.
  [parivartana-Ju-Me] Parivartana Yoga (Dainya)  planets=Me,Ju
      Mercury and Jupiter sit in each other's signs — a parivartana, or mutual exchange, linking the 3rd and 6th with the 9th and 12th. An exchange fuses two houses: neither can be read without the other, and whatever happens to one shows up in the other. A difficult house is inside the exchange, so its results arrive the hard way — obstruction first, resolution afterwards. The tradition still counts it a yoga: what it costs early it tends to return as capability later.
  [sunapha] Sunapha Yoga  planets=Ve
      Venus occupies the sign after the Moon. Sunapha builds self-made standing: intelligence applied to earning, wealth accumulated by the native's own effort rather than inherited, and a reputation that grows on results. What Venus governs is where that earning tends to concentrate.

--- personality ---
  headline: Aries rising, Sagittarius Moon in Mula, Capricorn Sun — a personality led by Sun and tested through Jupiter.
  strongest=Su weakest=Ju
  == Lagna — the body and the bearing
     [1] Aries rises at 23°58', in Bharani pada 4. The outward temperament is direct, competitive and quick to start — a pioneer's bearing that would rather act and correct than wait and be certain. Physical courage is real; patience is the lesson of the life.
     [2] The rising nakshatra colours the body and first impression: intense, transformative bearing; carries burdens and creative extremes.
     [3] The Lagna lord Mars sits in the 9th house in Sagittarius — Neutral Sign (Sama), composite strength 50/100 (Moderate). The life's centre of gravity therefore leans toward fortune: that agenda colours everything this chart attempts, and the native's sense of self is bound up in it.
     [4] Mars occupies Mula pada 1 (lord Ketu) — root-cutting inquiry; destroys to find foundational truth. Its nakshatra lord Ketu is neutral, transmitting this quality plainly and without colour.
     [5] Jupiter as 9th lord is the premier benefic; the Sun as 5th lord supports it. Mars protects as Lagna lord despite 8th lordship. Saturn (10th+11th), Mercury (3rd+6th) and Venus (2nd+7th, the maraka) require dignity before they deliver cleanly.
  == Chandra — the mind and its weather
     [1] The Moon is in Sagittarius in the 9th house — Enemy Sign (Shatru), composite strength 42/100 (Weak). The mind is optimistic and philosophical and needs room; feeling attaches itself to meaning and to freedom, and confinement — physical or moral — is the real distress.
     [2] Its nakshatra is Mula pada 4 (lord Ketu) — root-cutting inquiry; destroys to find foundational truth. This is the single most personal signature in the chart — it sets the Vimshottari dasha sequence and describes how the native actually experiences their own life from the inside. Its nakshatra lord Ketu is neutral, transmitting this quality plainly and without colour.
     [3] Placed in the 9th, the emotional life orbits fortune; that is where this native goes for comfort and where they are most easily unsettled.
  == Surya — the will and the self
     [1] The Sun is in Capricorn in the 10th house — Neutral Sign (Sama), composite strength 53/100 (Moderate). The will is disciplined and structural: identity rests on responsibility carried and authority earned.
     [2] In the 10th house, that identity is staked on career — this is the arena in which the native needs to matter, and failure here is felt as a failure of self rather than of circumstance.
     [3] The Sun occupies Shravana pada 1 (lord Moon) — attentive listening; learning, fame and connective intelligence. Its nakshatra lord Moon is a friend, so this quality comes through warmly and legibly.
  == The chart's strong and weak axis
     [1] Sun is the strongest graha in this chart at 53/100 (Moderate), placed in the 10th house in Capricorn. Its qualities are the ones this native reaches for by default and performs best under pressure — Sun's significations are where they are genuinely, reliably competent. Its top contributions are Dig bala (directional strength); House placement (10); Nakshatra lord Moon (Shravana) — friend.
     [2] Jupiter is the weakest at 13/100 (Afflicted), in the 3rd house in Gemini. This is the chart's soft flank: Jupiter's significations are where the native over-compensates, avoids, or quietly outsources to other people. Its heaviest drags are Dignity: greatEnemy; Aspect from Moon; Aspect from Mars.
     [3] The personality is largely the negotiation between these two. Growth in this chart looks less like adding to Sun — which needs no help — and more like refusing to let Jupiter's weakness set the terms, since it is precisely the area the native is most tempted to route around.
  == Atmakaraka — what this life is for
     [1] Saturn is the Atmakaraka (the soul significator — the planet that travelled furthest through its sign, at 24°37'). In simple terms: whatever Saturn stands for — placed in your 9th house in Sagittarius — is the lesson this life keeps returning to, in relationships, in work, everywhere. It is less "what you do" and more "what you cannot avoid becoming good at".
     [2] Its Karakamsa (the Navamsa sign of the Atmakaraka) is Scorpio: the inner room where that lesson is actually worked out. A Scorpio Karakamsa colours the soul's work: intense, private and penetrating, with feeling that runs deep and is rarely displayed. Loyalty is absolute and so is its withdrawal — this temperament does not do half-measures.
  == Navamsa — the inner person
     [1] The Navamsa (D-9) Lagna is Scorpio. If the birth chart is how life presents itself, the Navamsa is how you are on the inside once the noise settles — and a Scorpio inner nature is intense, private and penetrating, with feeling that runs deep and is rarely displayed. Loyalty is absolute and so is its withdrawal — this temperament does not do half-measures. When the outer (Aries) and inner (Scorpio) signs differ in element, people who only know you casually often misread you; those close to you meet the Navamsa.
     [2] Venus is Vargottama — occupying the same sign in both D-1 and D-9. A Vargottama planet keeps its promises: what it shows on the surface is what it actually is underneath, and it holds steady under pressure.
  == By the classical six-fold measure (Shadbala)
     [1] Measured the full classical way — position, direction, time of birth, motion, nature and aspects together — Sun is this chart's strongest planet at 9.57 rupas (147% of its required strength) and Moon the weakest at 5.08 rupas (85%). This agrees with the quick composite score above, which strengthens the reading.
     [2] Practically: lean on Sun's themes when stakes are high, and give Moon's themes more preparation time than feels necessary.
  == How you are seen vs how you are (Arudha Lagna)
     [1] The Arudha Lagna (how the world perceives you) falls in Leo, while the actual rising sign is Aries. The public image runs on Leo's wavelength — warm, proud and naturally centre-stage, where dignity is not vanity but a working requirement. Generous when respected, brittle when overlooked. — while the person underneath operates as Aries. The gap is not dishonesty; it is simply that reputation forms around the 5th house's themes. Knowing which conversations are about the image and which are about you is a lifelong advantage.
  == Yoga signatures in the personality
     [1] 3 combinations stamp themselves directly on the character: Gajakesari Yoga, Chandra-Mangala Yoga, Sunapha Yoga.
     [2] Gajakesari Yoga — Jupiter stands in a kendra from the Moon. The mind is backed by wisdom: reputation, counsel-worthiness and resilience in public life, strongest when Jupiter is dignified.
     [3] Chandra-Mangala Yoga — Moon and Mars conjoin — an earning combination: entrepreneurial drive fused with instinct. Wealth through self-effort, with a temper that needs channels, not suppression.
     [4] Sunapha Yoga — Venus occupies the sign after the Moon. Sunapha builds self-made standing: intelligence applied to earning, wealth accumulated by the native's own effort rather than inherited, and a reputation that grows on results. What Venus governs is where that earning tends to concentrate.
  == Functional weather — who works for this Lagna
     [1] Working for this Lagna: Jupiter in the 3rd, Great Enemy's Sign (Adhi Shatru), 13/100 (Afflicted); Sun in the 10th, Neutral Sign (Sama), 53/100 (Moderate); Mars in the 9th, Neutral Sign (Sama), 50/100 (Moderate). The traits these planets govern are the ones the native can lean on without second-guessing, and their dashas feel like the personality working as designed.
     [2] Working against it: Saturn in the 9th, Enemy Sign (Shatru), 42/100 (Weak); Mercury in the 9th, Enemy Sign (Shatru), 48/100 (Moderate); Venus in the 10th, Great Friend's Sign (Adhi Mitra), 45/100 (Moderate). These are not evil planets — they are the ones whose natural instincts pull against this particular rising sign's interests. Where they are strong, the native has a talent that repeatedly gets them into trouble; where weak, a blind spot they rarely notice.
     [3] Neutral: Moon in the 9th, Enemy Sign (Shatru), 42/100 (Weak) — these take their colour from whatever they associate with rather than carrying an agenda of their own.

--- houses ---
  == 1st House — Aries  occupied=false lord=Ma planets=[] aspects=[]
     [1] The 1st house falls in Aries and rules self, body, vitality, temperament and the overall direction of life. No graha occupies it, so the house is judged — as the classics prescribe — by the condition of its lord and by the drishti it receives.
     [2] The 1st is ruled by Mars, placed in the 9th house at 2°28' Sagittarius — Neutral Sign (Sama). Counted from the 1st itself, Mars stands in the 9th — a kendra or trikona from its own house, so the lord actively supports what it rules. Its placement carries the 1st's agenda into fortune — that is where these significations actually play out. Composite strength 50/100 (Moderate) — Mars can carry the 1st, but results here track the native's effort closely and are not gifted.
     [3] The lord Mars lies in Mula pada 1, ruled by Ketu — root-cutting inquiry; destroys to find foundational truth. Its nakshatra lord Ketu is neutral toward Mars: results are transmitted faithfully, but without amplification.
     [4] No graha casts drishti on the 1st. Its affairs run on the lord's condition alone, with neither external reinforcement nor interference — outcomes here are quieter and more self-determined than elsewhere in the chart.
     [5] In sum: with no occupant to speak for it, the 1st's fortunes rest on Mars's condition in the 9th and on the drishti above. Strengthen Mars and this house improves; nothing else reaches it directly.
  == 2nd House — Taurus  occupied=false lord=Ve planets=[] aspects=[Ra:5]
     [1] The 2nd house falls in Taurus and rules wealth, family, speech, food and accumulated resources. No graha occupies it, so the house is judged — as the classics prescribe — by the condition of its lord and by the drishti it receives.
     [2] The 2nd is ruled by Venus, placed in the 10th house at 1°42' Capricorn — Great Friend's Sign (Adhi Mitra), retrograde. Counted from the 2nd itself, Venus stands in the 9th — a kendra or trikona from its own house, so the lord actively supports what it rules. Its placement carries the 2nd's agenda into career — that is where these significations actually play out. Composite strength 45/100 (Moderate) — Venus can carry the 2nd, but results here track the native's effort closely and are not gifted.
     [3] The lord Venus lies in Uttara Ashadha pada 2, ruled by Sun — later, lasting victory; patient universal ambition. Its nakshatra lord Sun is a natural enemy of Venus, so even a well-placed Venus has its results filtered through an unsympathetic agent — expect the promise to arrive altered, later, or at a price.
     [4] Rahu aspects this house from the 10th in Capricorn (great friend's sign, retrograde) — a natural malefic and functionally neutral for this Lagna. The glance is its fifth drishti, Rahu's amplification of intelligence and desire. Rahu's aspect on the 2nd creates hunger around wealth, family and speech — accumulation is driven, sometimes through unorthodox or foreign channels, and the voice can persuade past the facts. Family history carries something unresolved. A strong malefic aspect does not merely afflict — it also forces competence in this house's affairs, exacting the discipline as its price.
     [5] Every drishti reaching this house is malefic. With no benefic counterweight, these significations need conscious defence, and the house tends to mature through difficulty rather than ease.
     [6] In sum: with no occupant to speak for it, the 2nd's fortunes rest on Venus's condition in the 10th and on the drishti above. Strengthen Venus and this house improves; nothing else reaches it directly.
  == 3rd House — Gemini  occupied=true lord=Me planets=[Ju] aspects=[Mo:7,Ma:7,Me:7,Sa:7]
     [1] The 3rd house falls in Gemini and rules courage, siblings, communication, skills and self-effort. Jupiter occupies it, so this house's affairs are lived directly rather than delegated.
     [2] The 3rd is ruled by Mercury, placed in the 9th house at 17°01' Sagittarius — Enemy Sign (Shatru). Counted from the 3rd itself, Mercury stands in the 7th — a kendra or trikona from its own house, so the lord actively supports what it rules. Its placement carries the 3rd's agenda into fortune — that is where these significations actually play out. Composite strength 48/100 (Moderate) — Mercury can carry the 3rd, but results here track the native's effort closely and are not gifted.
     [3] The lord Mercury lies in Purva Ashadha pada 2, ruled by Venus — invincible early victories; proud, philosophical, water-blessed. Its nakshatra lord Venus is a natural friend of Mercury, so these results are passed on willingly and arrive in recognisable form.
     [4] Jupiter at 8°42' Gemini: As lord of the 9th and 12th for Aries Lagna, Jupiter governs fortune and expenditure and operates as a functional benefic for this Lagna. Jupiter in the 3rd gives wise initiative — courage tempered by ethics. Siblings prosper or are themselves guru-figures. Writing and teaching flow naturally, though ambition here is softer than talent; effort must be deliberate. Jupiter is lodged with a great enemy — expect its significations to be taxed; remedial strengthening of this planet earns outsized returns. Jupiter is retrograde (vakri): its energy internalises and intensifies, revisiting its themes repeatedly across the life — matters conclude on the second or third pass, not the first.
     [5] Jupiter lies in Ardra pada 1, ruled by Rahu — stormy, penetrating intellect; breakthroughs after inner turbulence. Its nakshatra lord Rahu is neutral toward Jupiter: results are transmitted faithfully, but without amplification.
     [6] Moon aspects this house from the 9th in Sagittarius (enemy sign) — a natural malefic and functionally neutral for this Lagna. The glance is its full seventh drishti. Moonlight on the 3rd makes communication warm and instinctive and courage emotional rather than calculated; the native acts when moved. Siblings are close, and short journeys are undertaken on impulse. A weak malefic aspect afflicts without conferring much capability: friction here comes with little compensating discipline.
     [7] Mars aspects this house from the 9th in Sagittarius (neutral sign) — a natural malefic and a functional benefic for this Lagna. The glance is its full seventh drishti, direct and unavoidable.   == 4th House — Cancer  occupied=true lord=Mo planets=[Ke] aspects=[Su:7,Ma:8,Ve:7,Ra:7]
     [1] The 4th house falls in Cancer and rules home, mother, emotional foundations, property and inner contentment. Ketu occupies it, so this house's affairs are lived directly rather than delegated.
     [2] The 4th is ruled by Moon, placed in the 9th house at 10°14' Sagittarius — Enemy Sign (Shatru). Counted from the 4th itself, Moon stands in the 6th — a dusthana from its own house. The house's affairs are undermined from within, and repair here takes deliberate, sustained effort rather than good fortune. Its placement carries the 4th's agenda into fortune — that is where these significations actually play out. Composite strength is only 42/100 (Weak); the 4th is under-resourced at the lord level, and its affairs need conscious support before they will hold weight.
     [3] The lord Moon lies in Mula pada 4, ruled by Ketu — root-cutting inquiry; destroys to find foundational truth. Its nakshatra lord Ketu is neutral toward Moon: results are transmitted faithfully, but without amplification.
     [4] Ketu at 23°30' Cancer: Ketu owns no sign and acts through its dispositor, nakshatra lord and conjunctions — a karmic agent rather than a functional lord. The true homeland this native seeks has no postal code. Ketu is lodged with a great enemy — expect its significations to be taxed; remedial strengthening of this planet earns outsized returns. Ketu is retrograde (vakri): its energy internalises and intensifies, revisiting its themes repeatedly across the life — matters conclude on the second or third pass, not the first.
     [5] Ketu lies in Ashlesha pada 3, ruled by Mercury — coiled, hypnotic insight; strategy, secrecy and kundalini intensity. Its nakshatra lord Mercury is neutral toward Ketu: results are transmitted faithfully, but without amplification.
     [6] Sun aspects this house from the 10th in Capricorn (neutral sign) — a natural malefic and a functional benefic for this Lagna. The glance is its full seventh drishti.      [7] Mars aspects this house from the 9th in Sagittarius (neutral sign) — a natural malefic and a functional benefic for this Lagna.      [4] No graha casts drishti on the 5th. Its affairs run on the lord's condition alone, with neither external reinforcement nor interference — outcomes here are quieter and more self-determined than elsewhere in the chart.
     [5] In sum: with no occupant to speak for it, the 5th's fortunes rest on Sun's condition in the 10th and on the drishti above. Strengthen Sun and this house improves; nothing else reaches it directly.

     [3] The lord Jupiter lies in Ardra pada 1, ruled by Rahu — stormy, penetrating intellect; breakthroughs after inner turbulence. Its nakshatra lord Rahu is neutral toward Jupiter: results are transmitted faithfully, but without amplification.
     [4] Mars aspects this house from the 9th in Sagittarius (neutral sign) — a natural malefic and a functional benefic for this Lagna. The glance is its fourth drishti, Mars at close range and the most disruptive of its three aspects. Mars's gaze on the 12th burns resources in hidden places: expenditure on disputes, secret enemies, or exertion abroad. Well directed, it is the energy of the person who works hard where no one is watching; poorly directed, it is sleep lost to unfinished fights. As a malefic of moderate standing, its aspect keeps the house honest, applying pressure the native learns to work with. Its functional role softens this considerably — a natural malefic that works for this Lagna behaves far better than its reputation.
     [5] Ketu aspects this house from the 4th in Cancer (great enemy's sign, retrograde) — a natural malefic and functionally neutral for this Lagna. The glance is its ninth drishti, Ketu's refinement of dharma and fortune. Ketu's glance on the 12th is its most natural station: expenditure that quietly serves liberation, ease in solitude and in foreign lands, a vivid inner life in sleep. Among all the aspects, this is the strongest single indicator of moksha-orientation. A weak malefic aspect afflicts without conferring much capability: friction here comes with little compensating discipline.
     [6] Every drishti reaching this house is malefic. With no benefic counterweight, these significations need conscious defence, and the house tends to mature through difficulty rather than ease.
     [7] In sum: with no occupant to speak for it, the 12th's fortunes rest on Jupiter's condition in the 3rd and on the drishti above. Strengthen Jupiter and this house improves; nothing else reaches it directly.

--- section: marriage ---
  headline: Your marriage indications are mixed: warm significators on one side, some classical delay markers on the other. For a chart like this the windows matter far more than the average does.
  score=56 verdict=none confidence=75
  == The marriage houses and significators
     [1] Marriage is read from your 7th house and from the planets that naturally stand for partnership. Below are the two deeper views the tradition adds to that: the marriage pada, which describes the institution, and the Navamsa, which describes the daily life inside it.
     [2] Your Upapada Lagna (the marriage pada — the arudha of the 12th house) falls in Gemini, and Mercury rules it, sitting in your 9th house, Enemy Sign (Shatru). This point describes marriage as a standing institution in your life rather than as romance — the household, the in-laws, the public fact of being married. Its condition is the best single indicator of how settled and respected that arrangement tends to feel from the inside.
     [3] Your Navamsa (the D-9 divisional chart of marriage and inner strength) is where married life is actually read, and it says this: the Navamsa rising sign is Scorpio, ruled by Mars; its 7th house falls in Taurus, with nothing in it; and Venus lands in Capricorn, Vargottama (the same sign in both D-1 and D-9 — a mark of stability), which means what it promises tends to hold. Treat this as the private texture of the marriage — what the two of you are like at home on an ordinary Tuesday, rather than what the relationship looks like to everyone else.
     why(-1) Your 7th house is ruled by Venus, and it sits in your 10th house, Great Friend's Sign (Adhi Mitra), scoring 45/100 for strength. That is where your partnership life gets carried out — 10th-house matters and your marriage tend to move together.
     why(0) Venus is your natural significator for marriage, and it stands Great Friend's Sign (Adhi Mitra) in your 10th house (45/100). A significator in good condition shows up as ease in being close to someone: affection you can express, and a partnership that gets easier rather than heavier with time. [BPHS, kalatra karaka]
     why(2) Venus is your Darakaraka (the spouse significator — least advanced by degree), and it sits in your 10th house. The person you marry tends to carry that planet's signature — its temperament, its pace, its way of handling difficulty — closely enough that the description usually feels familiar once you meet them. [Jaimini Upadesa Sutras, chara karakas]
  == Mangal Dosha
     [1] Mars sits in your 9th house, which technically forms Mangal Dosha from the Moon — but your chart cancels it: Jupiter aspects Mars — the classical benefic override. That means the heat is there without the affliction the tradition warns about. In daily life it reads as directness rather than damage: you say the difficult thing early, which is usually kinder than the alternative.
  == What your partner tends to be like
     [1] Because Venus rules your 7th house, the person you marry tends to carry that planet's nature — you are drawn to it, and you recognise it quickly. Tradition also reads a direction from this, South-East of your birthplace, but hold that one loosely: direction rules are the softest technique on this page.
     [2] There are no strong foreign markers on your marriage axis, which leans the reading toward a partner from a familiar cultural circle. That is a tendency in the chart, not a boundary on your life — plenty of these charts marry across every kind of distance.
     [3] Venus, as your spouse significator, colours their temperament more than anything else here. Look for a strong Venus signature in whom you actually choose — it is usually more visible in how they behave under stress than in how they present at first.
  == What tradition offers, and what actually helps
     [1] The standard literature recommends strengthening Venus in the traditional way — Friday observances, white clothing, courtesy toward your partner. The last of those is the one that carries the weight: attention paid to the relationship is what the practice is really training.

  [nbrj-Ra] Neechabhanga Raja Yoga  planets=Ra
      Rahu's debilitation is cancelled because its dispositor Mars occupies a kendra from Lagna; and its dispositor stands in a kendra from the Moon; and Ketu, exalted in that very sign's field, occupies a kendra. What begins as the chart's weakest point matures into a source of unusual strength, typically after early-life struggle in that portfolio.
  [nbrj-Ke] Neechabhanga Raja Yoga  planets=Ke
      Ketu's debilitation is cancelled because Moon, exalted in that very sign's field, occupies a kendra; and its dispositor Venus is itself exalted. What begins as the chart's weakest point matures into a source of unusual strength, typically after early-life struggle in that portfolio.

      Moon and Mars conjoin — an earning combination: entrepreneurial drive fused with instinct. Wealth through self-effort, with a temper that needs channels, not suppression.
  [mahapurusha-Sa] Sasa Yoga (Pancha Mahapurusha)  planets=Sa
      Saturn is in its moolatrikona and occupies the 1st house, a kendra — one of the five Mahapurusha ("great person") combinations. It stamps the whole personality with formidable endurance and organisational authority built slowly and held for a long time. The native governs systems, labour and institutions, and outlasts flashier rivals. Coldness and a taste for control are the shadow.
  [raja-Me-Sa] Raja Yoga (Kendra–Trikona link)  planets=Sa,Me
      Saturn (an angle lord) and Mercury (a trine lord) conjoin. The classical engine of rank: the union of dharma and karma lords lifts status, and its results ripen in the dasha periods of the two planets involved.

  [dhana-9-11] Dhana Yoga (9th–11th lords)  planets=Ve,Ju

  [parivartana-Ju-Ve] Parivartana Yoga (Maha)  planets=Ju,Ve
      Jupiter and Venus sit in each other's signs — a parivartana, or mutual exchange, linking the 2nd and 11th with the 4th and 9th. An exchange fuses two houses: neither can be read without the other, and whatever happens to one shows up in the other. Both lords own auspicious houses, which is the strong form of the exchange: the two areas actively fund each other, and the native's periods for either planet tend to move both at once.
  [sunapha] Sunapha Yoga  planets=Ve
      Venus occupies the sign after the Moon. Sunapha builds self-made standing: intelligence applied to earning, wealth accumulated by the native's own effort rather than inherited, and a reputation that grows on results. What Venus governs is where that earning tends to concentrate.

--- personality ---
  headline: Aquarius rising, Aquarius Moon in Shatabhisha, Aquarius Sun — a personality led by Saturn and tested through Ketu.
  strongest=Sa weakest=Ke
  == Lagna — the body and the bearing
     [1] Aquarius rises at 15°28', in Shatabhisha pada 3. The outward temperament is detached, systemic and unconventionally principled, thinking in groups and futures rather than individuals and moments. Independent to a fault, and warm in a way that surprises people.
     [2] The rising nakshatra colours the body and first impression: veiled healing; solitary depths, mysticism and medicine.
     [3] The Lagna lord Saturn sits in the 1st house in Aquarius — Moolatrikona, composite strength 69/100 (Strong). The life's centre of gravity therefore leans toward self: that agenda colours everything this chart attempts, and the native's sense of self is bound up in it.
     [4] Saturn occupies Shatabhisha pada 2 (lord Rahu) — veiled healing; solitary depths, mysticism and medicine. Its nakshatra lord Rahu is neutral, transmitting this quality plainly and without colour.
     [5] Venus (4th+9th) is the yogakaraka; Saturn protects as Lagna+12th lord. Jupiter (2nd+11th) works as a maraka-flavoured accumulator, the Moon (6th) and Mars (3rd+10th) demand dignity before their periods reward.
  == Chandra — the mind and its weather
     [1] The Moon is in Aquarius in the 1st house — Enemy Sign (Shatru), composite strength 42/100 (Weak). The mind is detached and observational, feeling at one remove and more comfortable with humanity than with individuals. Emotional independence is real, occasionally to the point of remoteness.
     [2] Its nakshatra is Shatabhisha pada 2 (lord Rahu) — veiled healing; solitary depths, mysticism and medicine. This is the single most personal signature in the chart — it sets the Vimshottari dasha sequence and describes how the native actually experiences their own life from the inside. Its nakshatra lord Rahu is neutral, transmitting this quality plainly and without colour.
     [3] Placed in the 1st, the emotional life orbits self; that is where this native goes for comfort and where they are most easily unsettled.
  == Surya — the will and the self
     [1] The Sun is in Aquarius in the 1st house — Great Enemy's Sign (Adhi Shatru), composite strength 48/100 (Moderate). The will is independent and reforming: identity rests on principle rather than on approval.
     [2] In the 1st house, that identity is staked on self — this is the arena in which the native needs to matter, and failure here is felt as a failure of self rather than of circumstance.
     [3] The Sun occupies Purva Bhadrapada pada 2 (lord Jupiter) — fiery twin-front transformation; intensity behind an austere face. Its nakshatra lord Jupiter is a friend, so this quality comes through warmly and legibly.
  == The chart's strong and weak axis
     [1] Saturn is the strongest graha in this chart at 69/100 (Strong), placed in the 1st house in Aquarius. Its qualities are the ones this native reaches for by default and performs best under pressure — Saturn's significations are where they are genuinely, reliably competent. Its top contributions are Dignity: moolatrikona; House placement (1); Aspect from Jupiter.
     [2] Ketu is the weakest at 12/100 (Afflicted), in the 4th house in Taurus. This is the chart's soft flank: Ketu's significations are where the native over-compensates, avoids, or quietly outsources to other people. Its heaviest drags are Dignity: debilitated; Aspect from Mars; Aspect from Rahu.
     [3] The personality is largely the negotiation between these two. Growth in this chart looks less like adding to Saturn — which needs no help — and more like refusing to let Ketu's weakness set the terms, since it is precisely the area the native is most tempted to route around.
  == Atmakaraka — what this life is for
     [1] Sun is the Atmakaraka (the soul significator — the planet that travelled furthest through its sign, at 26°26'). In simple terms: whatever Sun stands for — placed in your 1st house in Aquarius — is the lesson this life keeps returning to, in relationships, in work, everywhere. It is less "what you do" and more "what you cannot avoid becoming good at".
     [2] Its Karakamsa (the Navamsa sign of the Atmakaraka) is Taurus: the inner room where that lesson is actually worked out. A Taurus Karakamsa colours the soul's work: steady, sensual and immovable once settled, built for accumulation and endurance rather than speed. Comfort is sought sincerely and change resisted instinctively.
  == Navamsa — the inner person
     [1] The Navamsa (D-9) Lagna is Aquarius. If the birth chart is how life presents itself, the Navamsa is how you are on the inside once the noise settles — and an Aquarius inner nature is detached, systemic and unconventionally principled, thinking in groups and futures rather than individuals and moments. Independent to a fault, and warm in a way that surprises people. When the outer (Aquarius) and inner (Aquarius) signs differ in element, people who only know you casually often misread you; those close to you meet the Navamsa.
  == By the classical six-fold measure (Shadbala)
     [1] Measured the full classical way — position, direction, time of birth, motion, nature and aspects together — Sun is this chart's strongest planet at 9.16 rupas (141% of its required strength) and Jupiter the weakest at 5.49 rupas (85%). Note this differs from the quick composite score above — the six-fold measure weighs birth-time and motion factors the composite does not; where they disagree, the Shadbala verdict is the classical one.
     [2] Practically: lean on Sun's themes when stakes are high, and give Jupiter's themes more preparation time than feels necessary.
  == Glances on the Lagna — who edits the personality
     [1] Jupiter casts its special 5th glance onto the Lagna — its fifth drishti, Jupiter's blessing on intelligence, counsel and progeny
     [2] Aspects on the first house edit the personality others meet: each glance above adds its colour on top of the rising sign itself.
  == How you are seen vs how you are (Arudha Lagna)
     [1] The Arudha Lagna (how the world perceives you) falls in Scorpio, while the actual rising sign is Aquarius. The public image runs on Scorpio's wavelength — intense, private and penetrating, with feeling that runs deep and is rarely displayed. Loyalty is absolute and so is its withdrawal — while the person underneath operates as Aquarius. The gap is not dishonesty; it is simply that reputation forms around the 10th house's themes. Knowing which conversations are about the image and which are about you is a lifelong advantage.
  == Yoga signatures in the personality
     [1] 4 combinations stamp themselves directly on the character: Budhaditya Yoga, Chandra-Mangala Yoga, Sasa Yoga (Pancha Mahapurusha), Sunapha Yoga.
     [2] Budhaditya Yoga — Sun and Mercury conjoin in the 1st house: sharp administrative intellect and articulate authority.
     [3] Chandra-Mangala Yoga — Moon and Mars conjoin — an earning combination: entrepreneurial drive fused with instinct. Wealth through self-effort, with a temper that needs channels, not suppression.
     [4] Sasa Yoga (Pancha Mahapurusha) — Saturn is in its moolatrikona and occupies the 1st house, a kendra — one of the five Mahapurusha ("great person") combinations. It stamps the whole personality with formidable endurance and organisational authority built slowly and held for a long time. The native governs systems, labour and institutions, and outlasts flashier rivals. Coldness and a taste for control are the shadow.
     [5] Sunapha Yoga — Venus occupies the sign after the Moon. Sunapha builds self-made standing: intelligence applied to earning, wealth accumulated by the native's own effort rather than inherited, and a reputation that grows on results. What Venus governs is where that earning tends to concentrate.
     [6] Beneath that sits a resilience signature — Neechabhanga Raja Yoga — the combinations that convert this chart's difficulties into capability rather than damage. They rarely show early; they are what the native is left holding after the hard decade.
     [7] Neechabhanga Raja Yoga — Rahu's debilitation is cancelled because its dispositor Mars occupies a kendra from Lagna; and its dispositor stands in a kendra from the Moon; and Ketu, exalted in that very sign's field, occupies a kendra. What begins as the chart's weakest point matures into a source of unusual strength, typically after early-life struggle in that portfolio.
     [8] Neechabhanga Raja Yoga — Ketu's debilitation is cancelled because Moon, exalted in that very sign's field, occupies a kendra; and its dispositor Venus is itself exalted. What begins as the chart's weakest point matures into a source of unusual strength, typically after early-life struggle in that portfolio.
  == Functional weather — who works for this Lagna
     [1] Venus is the yogakaraka for Aquarius Lagna — Venus in the 2nd, Exalted (Parama Ucha), 65/100 (Strong). Its condition is the single best predictor of how far this personality converts into standing in the world, because it rules a kendra and a trikona at once.
     [2] Working for this Lagna: Venus in the 2nd, Exalted (Parama Ucha), 65/100 (Strong); Saturn in the 1st, Moolatrikona, 69/100 (Strong). The traits these planets govern are the ones the native can lean on without second-guessing, and their dashas feel like the personality working as designed.
     [3] Working against it: Jupiter in the 9th, Great Enemy's Sign (Adhi Shatru), 47/100 (Moderate); Moon in the 1st, Enemy Sign (Shatru), 42/100 (Weak); Mars in the 1st, Enemy Sign (Shatru), 46/100 (Moderate). These are not evil planets — they are the ones whose natural instincts pull against this particular rising sign's interests. Where they are strong, the native has a talent that repeatedly gets them into trouble; where weak, a blind spot they rarely notice.
     [4] Neutral: Sun in the 1st, Great Enemy's Sign (Adhi Shatru), 48/100 (Moderate); Mercury in the 1st, Enemy Sign (Shatru), 54/100 (Moderate) — these take their colour from whatever they associate with rather than carrying an agenda of their own.

--- houses ---
  == 1st House — Aquarius  occupied=true lord=Sa planets=[Su,Mo,Ma,Me,Sa] aspects=[Ju:5]
     [1] The 1st house falls in Aquarius and rules self, body, vitality, temperament and the overall direction of life. Its combined occupancy makes this a major theatre of the life.
     [2] The 1st is ruled by Saturn, placed in the 1st house at 11°09' Aquarius — Moolatrikona. It sits in the very house it rules — the strongest possible endorsement of the 1st's promise, since the lord defends its own portfolio directly. Its placement carries the 1st's agenda into self — that is where these significations actually play out. With a composite strength of 69/100 (Strong), Saturn is in a position to deliver the 1st's significations rather than merely promise them.
     [3] The lord Saturn lies in Shatabhisha pada 2, ruled by Rahu — veiled healing; solitary depths, mysticism and medicine. Its nakshatra lord Rahu is neutral toward Saturn: results are transmitted faithfully, but without amplification.
     [4] Sun at 26°26' Aquarius: As lord of the 7th for Aquarius Lagna, Sun governs marriage and operates as functionally neutral for this Lagna, carrying maraka responsibility as well. The Sun on the Lagna gives an unmistakable presence — authority, vitality and a spine that does not bend easily. The body runs hot and the ego runs close to the surface; leadership comes naturally, but so does the tendency to dominate rooms that only needed a participant. Sun is lodged with a great enemy — expect its significations to be taxed; remedial strengthening of this planet earns outsized returns.
     [5] Sun lies in Purva Bhadrapada pada 2, ruled by Jupiter — fiery twin-front transformation; intensity behind an austere face. Its nakshatra lord Jupiter is a natural friend of Sun, so these results are passed on willingly and arrive in recognisable form.
     [6] Moon at 12°13' Aquarius: As lord of the 6th for Aquarius Lagna, Moon governs health and operates as a functional malefic for this Lagna. Moon on the Lagna makes the face of the chart emotional, responsive and public-facing. Moods are visible weather; the public likes this person without quite knowing why. Wellbeing tracks the Moon's dignity and phase closely. Moon sits in an enemy's sign; its agenda meets friction from the landlord, and results require roughly double the usual persistence.

  == 2nd House — Pisces  occupied=true lord=Ju planets=[Ve] aspects=[Ra:5]
     [1] The 2nd house falls in Pisces and rules wealth, family, speech, food and accumulated resources. Venus occupies it, so this house's affairs are lived directly rather than delegated.
     [2] The 2nd is ruled by Jupiter, placed in the 9th house at 20°42' Libra — Great Enemy's Sign (Adhi Shatru), retrograde. Counted from the 2nd itself, Jupiter stands in the 8th — a dusthana from its own house. The house's affairs are undermined from within, and repair here takes deliberate, sustained effort rather than good fortune. Its placement carries the 2nd's agenda into fortune — that is where these significations actually play out. Composite strength 47/100 (Moderate) — Jupiter can carry the 2nd, but results here track the native's effort closely and are not gifted.
     [3] The lord Jupiter lies in Vishakha pada 1, ruled by Jupiter — goal-fixated determination; twin ambitions pursued with fervour. Jupiter occupies its own nakshatra, so its intent reaches the world undiluted — what it promises, it delivers in its own voice.
     [4] Venus at 9°15' Pisces: As lord of the 4th and 9th for Aquarius Lagna, Venus governs home and fortune and operates as the yogakaraka for this Lagna. Venus in the 2nd sweetens wealth and speech alike: money through arts, luxury goods, finance and beauty industries. The voice may literally be musical. Family life is refined; the palate is expensive and knows it. Venus is exalted here — its significations operate at full, sometimes overwhelming, capacity; this is one of the chart's power centres.
     [5] Venus lies in Uttara Bhadrapada pada 2, ruled by Saturn — deep-sea serenity; wisdom raised from the depths, slow and profound. Its nakshatra lord Saturn is a natural friend of Venus, so these results are passed on willingly and arrive in recognisable form.
     [6] Rahu aspects this house from the 10th in Scorpio (debilitated, retrograde) — a natural malefic and functionally neutral for this Lagna. The glance is its fifth drishti, Rahu's amplification of intelligence and desire. Rahu's aspect on the 2nd creates hunger around wealth, family and speech — accumulation is driven, sometimes through unorthodox or foreign channels, and the voice can persuade past the facts. Family history carries something unresolved. A weak malefic aspect afflicts without conferring much capability: friction here comes with little compensating discipline.
     [7] Every drishti reaching this house is malefic. With no benefic counterweight, these significations need conscious defence, and the house tends to mature through difficulty rather than ease.

Its placement carries the 10th's agenda into self — that is where these significations actually play out. Composite strength 46/100 (Moderate) — Mars can carry the 10th, but results here track the native's effort closely and are not gifted.
     [3] The lord Mars lies in Shatabhisha pada 1, ruled by Rahu — veiled healing; solitary depths, mysticism and medicine. Its nakshatra lord Rahu is neutral toward Mars: results are transmitted faithfully, but without amplification.
     [4] Rahu at 3°39' Scorpio: Rahu owns no sign and acts through its dispositor, nakshatra lord and conjunctions — a karmic agent rather than a functional lord. Rahu in the 10th is the fame algorithm: obsessive career ambition, mass-market instincts, success through technology, media or foreign systems. Reputation inflates fast — and needs ethical ballast to avoid equally fast correction. Rahu is debilitated (neecha): its confidence is structurally undermined and its portfolios need conscious, repeated shoring up — unless a Neechabhanga cancellation (checked in the yoga findings) converts the weakness into eventual strength. Rahu is retrograde (vakri): its energy internalises and intensifies, revisiting its themes repeatedly across the life — matters conclude on the second or third pass, not the first. Note (Bhava Chalit): by Sripati cusps this planet actually operates from the 9th bhava — read its concrete, event-level results there, while its sign-based dignity stays as above.
     [5] Rahu lies in Anuradha pada 1, ruled by Saturn — loyal, disciplined devotion; friendship as a spiritual force. Its nakshatra lord Saturn is a natural friend of Rahu, so these results are passed on willingly and arrive in recognisable form.
     [6] Saturn aspects this house from the 1st in Aquarius (in moolatrikona) — a natural malefic and a functional benefic for this Lagna. The glance is its tenth drishti, the aspect of imposed duty and scrutiny. Saturn on the 10th carries its directional strength by signification, and is one of the strongest career glances in the chart: authority through service, seniority through endurance, and a reputation for reliability that compounds. Advancement is slow and essentially irreversible. A strong malefic aspect does not merely afflict — it also forces competence in this house's affairs, exacting the discipline as its price. Its functional role softens this considerably — a natural malefic that works for this Lagna behaves far better than its reputation.
     [7] Ketu aspects this house from the 4th in Taurus (debilitated, retrograde) — a natural malefic and functionally neutral for this Lagna. The glance is its full seventh drishti, Ketu's direct detachment. Ketu on the 10th gives a career pursued without hunger for its rewards, often technical, healing or spiritual, and a public standing the native is oddly indifferent to. Ambition arrives late, if at all, and the work is better for it. A weak malefic aspect afflicts without conferring much capability: friction here comes with little compensating discipline.
     [8] Every drishti reaching this house is malefic. With no benefic counterweight, these significations need conscious defence, and the house tends to mature through difficulty rather than ease.
  == 11th House — Sagittarius  occupied=false lord=Ju planets=[] aspects=[]
     [1] The 11th house falls in Sagittarius and rules gains, income, networks, elder siblings and fulfilment of desires. No graha occupies it, so the house is judged — as the classics prescribe — by the condition of its lord and by the drishti it receives.
     [2] The 11th is ruled by Jupiter, placed in the 9th house at 20°42' Libra — Great Enemy's Sign (Adhi Shatru), retrograde. Counted from the 11th itself, Jupiter stands in the 11th, a working but unremarkable station from its own house. Its placement carries the 11th's agenda into fortune — that is where these significations actually play out. Composite strength 47/100 (Moderate) — Jupiter can carry the 11th, but results here track the native's effort closely and are not gifted.
     [3] The lord Jupiter lies in Vishakha pada 1, ruled by Jupiter — goal-fixated determination; twin ambitions pursued with fervour. Jupiter occupies its own nakshatra, so its intent reaches the world undiluted — what it promises, it delivers in its own voice.
     [4] No graha casts drishti on the 11th. Its affairs run on the lord's condition alone, with neither external reinforcement nor interference — outcomes here are quieter and more self-determined than elsewhere in the chart.
     [5] In sum: with no occupant to speak for it, the 11th's fortunes rest on Jupiter's condition in the 9th and on the drishti above. Strengthen Jupiter and this house improves; nothing else reaches it directly.
  == 12th House — Capricorn  occupied=false lord=Sa planets=[] aspects=[Ke:9]
     [1] The 12th house falls in Capricorn and rules expenditure, losses, foreign lands, isolation, sleep and moksha. No graha occupies it, so the house is judged — as the classics prescribe — by the condition of its lord and by the drishti it receives.
     [2] The 12th is ruled by Saturn, placed in the 1st house at 11°09' Aquarius — Moolatrikona.      [3] The lord Saturn lies in Shatabhisha pada 2, ruled by Rahu — veiled healing; solitary depths, mysticism and medicine. Its nakshatra lord Rahu is neutral toward Saturn: results are transmitted faithfully, but without amplification.

     [3] Your Navamsa (the D-9 divisional chart of marriage and inner strength) is where married life is actually read, and it says this: the Navamsa rising sign is Aquarius, ruled by Saturn; its 7th house falls in Leo, with nothing in it; and Venus lands in Virgo. Treat this as the private texture of the marriage — what the two of you are like at home on an ordinary Tuesday, rather than what the relationship looks like to everyone else.

  == Mangal Dosha
     [1] Mars sits in your 1st house, which technically forms Mangal Dosha from the Lagna and from the Moon — but your chart cancels it: Jupiter aspects Mars — the classical benefic override. That means the heat is there without the affliction the tradition warns about. In daily life it reads as directness rather than damage: you say the difficult thing early, which is usually kinder than the alternative.
  == What your partner tends to be like
     [1] Because Sun rules your 7th house, the person you marry tends to carry that planet's nature — you are drawn to it, and you recognise it quickly.      [2] There are no strong foreign markers on your marriage axis, which leans the reading toward a partner from a familiar cultural circle. That is a tendency in the chart, not a boundary on your life — plenty of these charts marry across every kind of distance.
     [3] Mercury, as your spouse significator, colours their temperament more than anything else here. Look for a strong Mercury signature in whom you actually choose — it is usually more visible in how they behave under stress than in how they present at first.
  == What tradition offers, and what actually helps
     [1] The standard literature recommends strengthening Venus in the traditional way — Friday observances, white clothing, courtesy toward your partner. The last of those is the one that carries the weight: attention paid to the relationship is what the practice is really training.

--- yogas ---
  [nbrj-Su] Neechabhanga Raja Yoga  planets=Su
      Sun's debilitation is cancelled because Saturn, exalted in that very sign's field, occupies a kendra. What begins as the chart's weakest point matures into a source of unusual strength, typically after early-life struggle in that portfolio.
  [nbrj-Mo] Neechabhanga Raja Yoga  planets=Mo
      Moon's debilitation is cancelled because its dispositor Mars occupies a kendra from Lagna; and its dispositor stands in a kendra from the Moon. What begins as the chart's weakest point matures into a source of unusual strength, typically after early-life struggle in that portfolio.
  [vrj-Ve] Vipareeta Raja Yoga (Harsha)  planets=Ve
      Venus, lord of the 6th, sits in the 6th — a dusthana lord hidden in a dusthana. Adversity turns on itself: rivals self-destruct, crises resolve in the native's favour, and gains arrive through difficulty others cannot stomach.
  [chandra-mangala] Chandra-Mangala Yoga  planets=Mo,Ma
      Moon and Mars conjoin — an earning combination: entrepreneurial drive fused with instinct. Wealth through self-effort, with a temper that needs channels, not suppression.
  [mahapurusha-Ma] Ruchaka Yoga (Pancha Mahapurusha)  planets=Ma
      Mars is in its own sign and occupies the 7th house, a kendra — one of the five Mahapurusha ("great person") combinations. It stamps the whole personality with a commanding, athletic physicality and fearless executive nerve. The native leads from the front, thrives in competition, defence, surgery or engineering, and carries authority that is taken rather than granted. Anger is the tax on the gift.
  [yk-Sa] Yogakaraka in Strength  planets=Sa
      Saturn, the yogakaraka for this Lagna (simultaneous kendra and trikona lord), occupies the 4th house. A single-planet raja yoga engine: its dasha periods carry the chart's biggest promotions in status and material standing.
  [raja-Ma-Me] Raja Yoga (Kendra–Trikona link)  planets=Ma,Me
      Mars (an angle lord) and Mercury (a trine lord) conjoin. The classical engine of rank: the union of dharma and karma lords lifts status, and its results ripen in the dasha periods of the two planets involved.
  [anapha] Anapha Yoga  planets=Ve
      Venus occupies the sign before the Moon. Anapha gives a settled constitution and an agreeable presence: health that holds, comfort that is enjoyed rather than chased, and a good name that arrives without campaigning for it. A pull toward simplicity often shows in the second half of life.

--- personality ---
  headline: Taurus rising, Scorpio Moon in Anuradha, Libra Sun — a personality led by Jupiter and tested through Sun.
  strongest=Ju weakest=Su
  == Lagna — the body and the bearing
     [1] Taurus rises at 8°01', in Krittika pada 4. The outward temperament is steady, sensual and immovable once settled, built for accumulation and endurance rather than speed. Comfort is sought sincerely and change resisted instinctively.
     [2] The rising nakshatra colours the body and first impression: sharp, purifying fire; cuts through falsehood, ambitious and critical.
     [3] The Lagna lord Venus sits in the 6th house in Libra — Own Sign (Swarashi), retrograde, composite strength 53/100 (Moderate). The life's centre of gravity therefore leans toward health: that agenda colours everything this chart attempts, and the native's sense of self is bound up in it.
     [4] Venus occupies Vishakha pada 2 (lord Jupiter) — goal-fixated determination; twin ambitions pursued with fervour. Its nakshatra lord Jupiter is neutral, transmitting this quality plainly and without colour.
     [5] Saturn, lord of the 9th and 10th, is the textbook yogakaraka — its strong periods build career and fortune together. Jupiter (8th+11th) and the Moon (3rd) work against the grain; Mars carries maraka charge as 7th lord.
  == Chandra — the mind and its weather
     [1] The Moon is in Scorpio in the 7th house — Debilitated (Neecha), composite strength 23/100 (Afflicted). The mind runs deep, secretive and intense, with feelings neither displayed nor discarded. This is the Moon's hardest station and the compensation is formidable psychological depth.
     [2] Its nakshatra is Anuradha pada 2 (lord Saturn) — loyal, disciplined devotion; friendship as a spiritual force. This is the single most personal signature in the chart — it sets the Vimshottari dasha sequence and describes how the native actually experiences their own life from the inside. Its nakshatra lord Saturn is neutral, transmitting this quality plainly and without colour.
     [3] Placed in the 7th, the emotional life orbits marriage; that is where this native goes for comfort and where they are most easily unsettled.
  == Surya — the will and the self
     [1] The Sun is in Libra in the 6th house — Debilitated (Neecha), composite strength 11/100 (Afflicted). The will is relational and consultative: identity is negotiated rather than asserted, which costs the Sun its natural authority but produces genuine fairness.
     [2] In the 6th house, that identity is staked on health — this is the arena in which the native needs to matter, and failure here is felt as a failure of self rather than of circumstance.
     [3] The Sun occupies Swati pada 3 (lord Rahu) — independent, wind-borne flexibility; scatters then finds its own path. Its nakshatra lord Rahu is neutral, transmitting this quality plainly and without colour.
  == The chart's strong and weak axis
     [1] Jupiter is the strongest graha in this chart at 64/100 (Strong), placed in the 3rd house in Cancer. Its qualities are the ones this native reaches for by default and performs best under pressure — Jupiter's significations are where they are genuinely, reliably competent. Its top contributions are Dignity: exalted; Ashtakavarga 6 bindus in sign.
     [2] Sun is the weakest at 11/100 (Afflicted), in the 6th house in Libra. This is the chart's soft flank: Sun's significations are where the native over-compensates, avoids, or quietly outsources to other people. Its heaviest drags are Dignity: debilitated; House placement (6); Aspect from Saturn.
     [3] The personality is largely the negotiation between these two. Growth in this chart looks less like adding to Jupiter — which needs no help — and more like refusing to let Sun's weakness set the terms, since it is precisely the area the native is most tempted to route around.
  == Atmakaraka — what this life is for
     [1] Venus is the Atmakaraka (the soul significator — the planet that travelled furthest through its sign, at 24°45'). In simple terms: whatever Venus stands for — placed in your 6th house in Libra — is the lesson this life keeps returning to, in relationships, in work, everywhere. It is less "what you do" and more "what you cannot avoid becoming good at".
     [2] Its Karakamsa (the Navamsa sign of the Atmakaraka) is Taurus: the inner room where that lesson is actually worked out. A Taurus Karakamsa colours the soul's work: steady, sensual and immovable once settled, built for accumulation and endurance rather than speed. Comfort is sought sincerely and change resisted instinctively.
  == Navamsa — the inner person
     [1] The Navamsa (D-9) Lagna is Pisces. If the birth chart is how life presents itself, the Navamsa is how you are on the inside once the noise settles — and a Pisces inner nature is impressionable, compassionate and porous to atmosphere, absorbing whatever surrounds it and needing boundaries it is reluctant to build. Imagination and faith are the standing strengths. When the outer (Taurus) and inner (Pisces) signs differ in element, people who only know you casually often misread you; those close to you meet the Navamsa.
  == By the classical six-fold measure (Shadbala)
     [1] Measured the full classical way — position, direction, time of birth, motion, nature and aspects together — Jupiter is this chart's strongest planet at 9.25 rupas (142% of its required strength) and Venus the weakest at 5.59 rupas (102%). This agrees with the quick composite score above, which strengthens the reading.
     [2] Practically: lean on Jupiter's themes when stakes are high, and give Venus's themes more preparation time than feels necessary.
  == Glances on the Lagna — who edits the personality
     [1] Moon casts its 7th glance onto the Lagna — its full seventh drishti
     [2] Mars casts its 7th glance onto the Lagna — its full seventh drishti, direct and unavoidable
     [3] Mercury casts its 7th glance onto the Lagna — its full seventh drishti
     [4] Saturn casts its special 10th glance onto the Lagna — its tenth drishti, the aspect of imposed duty and scrutiny
     [5] Rahu casts its special 9th glance onto the Lagna — its ninth drishti, Rahu's distortion of inherited fortune and belief
     [6] Aspects on the first house edit the personality others meet: each glance above adds its colour on top of the rising sign itself.
  == How you are seen vs how you are (Arudha Lagna)
     [1] The Arudha Lagna (how the world perceives you) falls in Pisces, while the actual rising sign is Taurus. The public image runs on Pisces's wavelength — impressionable, compassionate and porous to atmosphere, absorbing whatever surrounds it and needing boundaries it is reluctant to build. Imagination and faith are the standing strengths. — while the person underneath operates as Taurus. The gap is not dishonesty; it is simply that reputation forms around the 11th house's themes. Knowing which conversations are about the image and which are about you is a lifelong advantage.
  == Yoga signatures in the personality
     [1] 4 combinations stamp themselves directly on the character: Chandra-Mangala Yoga, Ruchaka Yoga (Pancha Mahapurusha), Yogakaraka in Strength, Anapha Yoga.
     [2] Chandra-Mangala Yoga — Moon and Mars conjoin — an earning combination: entrepreneurial drive fused with instinct. Wealth through self-effort, with a temper that needs channels, not suppression.
     [3] Ruchaka Yoga (Pancha Mahapurusha) — Mars is in its own sign and occupies the 7th house, a kendra — one of the five Mahapurusha ("great person") combinations. It stamps the whole personality with a commanding, athletic physicality and fearless executive nerve. The native leads from the front, thrives in competition, defence, surgery or engineering, and carries authority that is taken rather than granted. Anger is the tax on the gift.
     [4] Yogakaraka in Strength — Saturn, the yogakaraka for this Lagna (simultaneous kendra and trikona lord), occupies the 4th house. A single-planet raja yoga engine: its dasha periods carry the chart's biggest promotions in status and material standing.
     [5] Anapha Yoga — Venus occupies the sign before the Moon. Anapha gives a settled constitution and an agreeable presence: health that holds, comfort that is enjoyed rather than chased, and a good name that arrives without campaigning for it. A pull toward simplicity often shows in the second half of life.
     [6] Beneath that sits a resilience signature — Neechabhanga Raja Yoga, Vipareeta Raja Yoga (Harsha) — the combinations that convert this chart's difficulties into capability rather than damage. They rarely show early; they are what the native is left holding after the hard decade.
     [7] Neechabhanga Raja Yoga — Sun's debilitation is cancelled because Saturn, exalted in that very sign's field, occupies a kendra. What begins as the chart's weakest point matures into a source of unusual strength, typically after early-life struggle in that portfolio.
     [8] Neechabhanga Raja Yoga — Moon's debilitation is cancelled because its dispositor Mars occupies a kendra from Lagna; and its dispositor stands in a kendra from the Moon. What begins as the chart's weakest point matures into a source of unusual strength, typically after early-life struggle in that portfolio.
     [9] Vipareeta Raja Yoga (Harsha) — Venus, lord of the 6th, sits in the 6th — a dusthana lord hidden in a dusthana. Adversity turns on itself: rivals self-destruct, crises resolve in the native's favour, and gains arrive through difficulty others cannot stomach.
  == Functional weather — who works for this Lagna
     [1] Saturn is the yogakaraka for Taurus Lagna — Saturn in the 4th, Neutral Sign (Sama), 52/100 (Moderate). Its condition is the single best predictor of how far this personality converts into standing in the world, because it rules a kendra and a trikona at once.
     [2] Working for this Lagna: Saturn in the 4th, Neutral Sign (Sama), 52/100 (Moderate); Mercury in the 7th, Enemy Sign (Shatru), 34/100 (Weak); Sun in the 6th, Debilitated (Neecha), 11/100 (Afflicted). The traits these planets govern are the ones the native can lean on without second-guessing, and their dashas feel like the personality working as designed.
     [3] Working against it: Moon in the 7th, Debilitated (Neecha), 23/100 (Afflicted); Jupiter in the 3rd, Exalted (Parama Ucha), 64/100 (Strong); Mars in the 7th, Own Sign (Swarashi), 58/100 (Moderate). These are not evil planets — they are the ones whose natural instincts pull against this particular rising sign's interests. Where they are strong, the native has a talent that repeatedly gets them into trouble; where weak, a blind spot they rarely notice.
     [4] Neutral: Venus in the 6th, Own Sign (Swarashi), 53/100 (Moderate) — these take their colour from whatever they associate with rather than carrying an agenda of their own.

  == 1st House — Taurus  occupied=false lord=Ve planets=[] aspects=[Mo:7,Ma:7,Me:7,Sa:10,Ra:9]
     [1] The 1st house falls in Taurus and rules self, body, vitality, temperament and the overall direction of life. No graha occupies it, so the house is judged — as the classics prescribe — by the condition of its lord and by the drishti it receives.
     [2] The 1st is ruled by Venus, placed in the 6th house at 24°45' Libra — Own Sign (Swarashi), retrograde. Counted from the 1st itself, Venus stands in the 6th — a dusthana from its own house. The house's affairs are undermined from within, and repair here takes deliberate, sustained effort rather than good fortune. Its placement carries the 1st's agenda into health — that is where these significations actually play out. Composite strength 53/100 (Moderate) — Venus can carry the 1st, but results here track the native's effort closely and are not gifted.
     [3] The lord Venus lies in Vishakha pada 2, ruled by Jupiter — goal-fixated determination; twin ambitions pursued with fervour. Its nakshatra lord Jupiter is neutral toward Venus: results are transmitted faithfully, but without amplification.
     [4] Moon aspects this house from the 7th in Scorpio (debilitated) — a natural benefic and a functional malefic for this Lagna. The glance is its full seventh drishti. The Moon's gaze on the Lagna gives an approachable, responsive temperament and a face people find easy to trust; the body and the mood, however, run on tides rather than on a schedule. The benefic intent is present but under-resourced: the protection is real yet thin, and arrives later than the native expects. Note the split, though: naturally benefic but functionally adverse for this Lagna, so its blessing here arrives with strings attached.
     [5] Mars aspects this house from the 7th in Scorpio (own sign) — a natural malefic and a functional malefic for this Lagna. The glance is its full seventh drishti, direct and unavoidable. Mars's drishti on the Lagna gives physical courage, a competitive edge and a body built for exertion, along with a temper that arrives before the reasoning does. Scars, literal and otherwise, are collected early. A strong malefic aspect does not merely afflict — it also forces competence in this house's affairs, exacting the discipline as its price.
     [6] Mercury aspects this house from the 7th in Scorpio (enemy sign) — a natural malefic and a functional benefic for this Lagna. The glance is its full seventh drishti. Mercury's gaze on the Lagna gives a youthful, quick-witted presence and a nervous system that runs on information; the native is articulate long before they are settled. Adaptability is the strength, dispersion the risk. A weak malefic aspect afflicts without conferring much capability: friction here comes with little compensating discipline. Its functional role softens this considerably — a natural malefic that works for this Lagna behaves far better than its reputation.
     [7] Saturn aspects this house from the 4th in Leo (neutral sign) — a natural malefic and the yogakaraka for this Lagna. The glance is its tenth drishti, the aspect of imposed duty and scrutiny. Saturn's drishti on the Lagna sobers the temperament and slows the body's early flowering; the native seems older than their years and comes into their own late. What is built on this foundation does not fall down. As a malefic of moderate standing, its aspect keeps the house honest, applying pressure the native learns to work with. As the yogakaraka for this Lagna, its glance is among the chart's best structural supports wherever it lands.
     [8] Rahu aspects this house from the 5th in Virgo (great friend's sign, retrograde) — a natural malefic and functionally neutral for this Lagna. The glance is its ninth drishti, Rahu's distortion of inherited fortune and belief. Rahu's drishti on the Lagna amplifies the persona and blurs its edges: unusual magnetism, an unconventional self-presentation, and an identity assembled rather than inherited. Other people's projections stick to this native easily. A strong malefic aspect does not merely afflict — it also forces competence in this house's affairs, exacting the discipline as its price.
     [9] The house takes 1 benefic and 4 malefic aspects — a contested field whose results swing with whichever of these planets is running its dasha.
     [10] In sum: with no occupant to speak for it, the 1st's fortunes rest on Venus's condition in the 6th and on the drishti above. Strengthen Venus and this house improves; nothing else reaches it directly.

Note the split, though: naturally benefic but functionally adverse for this Lagna, so its blessing here arrives with strings attached.
     [5] Rahu aspects this house from the 5th in Virgo (great friend's sign, retrograde) — a natural malefic and functionally neutral for this Lagna. The glance is its fifth drishti, Rahu's amplification of intelligence and desire. Rahu's aspect on the 9th unsettles inherited belief — unorthodox philosophy, foreign gurus, and a dharma the native has to build rather than receive. The father-signification carries distance or disruption. A strong malefic aspect does not merely afflict — it also forces competence in this house's affairs, exacting the discipline as its price.
     [6] The house takes 1 benefic and 1 malefic aspect — a contested field whose results swing with whichever of these planets is running its dasha.
     [7] In sum: with no occupant to speak for it, the 9th's fortunes rest on Saturn's condition in the 4th and on the drishti above. Strengthen Saturn and this house improves; nothing else reaches it directly.
  == 10th House — Aquarius  occupied=false lord=Sa planets=[] aspects=[Ma:4,Sa:7]
     [1] The 10th house falls in Aquarius and rules career, status, authority, karma and public achievement. No graha occupies it, so the house is judged — as the classics prescribe — by the condition of its lord and by the drishti it receives.
     [2] The 10th is ruled by Saturn, placed in the 4th house at 17°59' Leo — Neutral Sign (Sama). Counted from the 10th itself, Saturn stands in the 7th — a kendra or trikona from its own house, so the lord actively supports what it rules. Its placement carries the 10th's agenda into home — that is where these significations actually play out. Composite strength 52/100 (Moderate) — Saturn can carry the 10th, but results here track the native's effort closely and are not gifted.
     [3] The lord Saturn lies in Purva Phalguni pada 2, ruled by Venus — pleasure-loving warmth; charm, patronage and creative enjoyment. Its nakshatra lord Venus is a natural friend of Saturn, so these results are passed on willingly and arrive in recognisable form.
     [4] Mars aspects this house from the 7th in Scorpio (own sign) — a natural malefic and a functional malefic for this Lagna. The glance is its fourth drishti, Mars at close range and the most disruptive of its three aspects. Mars on the 10th carries its directional strength by signification: driving ambition, executive force, and a career built on decisive action — defence, surgery, engineering, sport, enterprise. Authority is seized rather than awaited, and subordinates feel the heat. A strong malefic aspect does not merely afflict — it also forces competence in this house's affairs, exacting the discipline as its price.
     [5] Saturn aspects this house from the 4th in Leo (neutral sign) — a natural malefic and the yogakaraka for this Lagna. The glance is its full seventh drishti, Saturn's direct weight and the one that delays. Saturn on the 10th carries its directional strength by signification, and is one of the strongest career glances in the chart: authority through service, seniority through endurance, and a reputation for reliability that compounds. Advancement is slow and essentially irreversible. As a malefic of moderate standing, its aspect keeps the house honest, applying pressure the native learns to work with. As the yogakaraka for this Lagna, its glance is among the chart's best structural supports wherever it lands.
     [6] Every drishti reaching this house is malefic. With no benefic counterweight, these significations need conscious defence, and the house tends to mature through difficulty rather than ease.
     [7] In sum: with no occupant to speak for it, the 10th's fortunes rest on Saturn's condition in the 4th and on the drishti above. Strengthen Saturn and this house improves; nothing else reaches it directly.

  == The marriage houses and significators
     [1] Marriage is read from your 7th house and from the planets that naturally stand for partnership. Below are the two deeper views the tradition adds to that: the marriage pada, which describes the institution, and the Navamsa, which describes the daily life inside it.
     [2] Your Upapada Lagna (the marriage pada — the arudha of the 12th house) falls in Gemini, and Mercury rules it, sitting in your 7th house, Enemy Sign (Shatru). Its condition is the best single indicator of how settled and respected that arrangement tends to feel from the inside.
     [3] Your Navamsa (the D-9 divisional chart of marriage and inner strength) is where married life is actually read, and it says this: the Navamsa rising sign is Pisces, ruled by Jupiter; its 7th house falls in Virgo, with Moon, Mars, Saturn there; and Venus lands in Taurus. Treat this as the private texture of the marriage — what the two of you are like at home on an ordinary Tuesday, rather than what the relationship looks like to everyone else.

  == Mangal Dosha
     [1] Mars sits in your 7th house, which technically forms Mangal Dosha from the Lagna and from the Moon — but your chart cancels it: Mars stands in its own sign (Scorpio) — a dignified Mars is held not to afflict; Jupiter aspects Mars — the classical benefic override. That means the heat is there without the affliction the tradition warns about. In daily life it reads as directness rather than damage: you say the difficult thing early, which is usually kinder than the alternative.
  == What your partner tends to be like

     [3] Mercury, as your spouse significator, colours their temperament more than anything else here. Look for a strong Mercury signature in whom you actually choose — it is usually more visible in how they behave under stress than in how they present at first.
  == What tradition offers, and what actually helps
     [1] The standard literature recommends strengthening Venus in the traditional way — Friday observances, white clothing, courtesy toward your partner. The last of those is the one that carries the weight: attention paid to the relationship is what the practice is really training.
