--- yogas ---
  [budhaditya] Budhaditya Yoga  planets=Su,Me
      Sun and Mercury conjoin in the 3rd house: sharp administrative intellect and articulate authority, though Mercury's combustion demands the native learn to separate ego from analysis.
  [chandra-mangala] Chandra-Mangala Yoga  planets=Mo,Ma
      Moon and Mars conjoin — an earning combination: entrepreneurial drive fused with instinct. Wealth through self-effort, with a temper that needs channels, not suppression.
  [raja-Sa-Ve] Raja Yoga (Kendra–Trikona link)  planets=Ve,Sa
      Venus (an angle lord) and Saturn (a trine lord) stand in mutual aspect. The classical engine of rank: the union of dharma and karma lords lifts status, and its results ripen in the dasha periods of the two planets involved.
  [dhana-9-11] Dhana Yoga (9th–11th lords)  planets=Me,Su
      The 9th lord Mercury joins the 11th lord Sun — fortune's lord touching the wealth axis. Money arrives through merit, timing or backing rather than grind alone.
  [parivartana-Ju-Me] Parivartana Yoga (Dainya)  planets=Ju,Me
      Jupiter and Mercury sit in each other's signs — a parivartana, or mutual exchange, linking the 3rd and 6th with the 9th and 12th. An exchange fuses two houses: neither can be read without the other, and whatever happens to one shows up in the other. A difficult house is inside the exchange, so its results arrive the hard way — obstruction first, resolution afterwards. The tradition still counts it a yoga: what it costs early it tends to return as capability later.
  [kemadruma] Kemadruma Yoga (cancelled)  planets=Mo
      The Moon has no planetary neighbour in the signs beside it — Kemadruma forms — but it is cancelled because planets occupy kendras from the Moon. The isolation shows only as an occasional need for solitude, not as the classical loneliness of the yoga.
  [amala-moon] Amala Yoga  planets=Ve
      Only natural benefics (Venus) occupy the 10th from the Moon. The "stainless" yoga: reputation stays clean, work is respected, and standing survives controversy. Career fortunes rise on merit rather than manoeuvre.

--- personality ---
  headline: Libra rising, Aquarius Moon in Shatabhisha, Sagittarius Sun — a personality led by Moon and tested through Jupiter.
  strongest=Mo weakest=Ju
  == Lagna — the body and the bearing
     [1] Libra rises at 0°49', in Chitra pada 3. The outward temperament is gracious, balancing and relationship-oriented; this native thinks by consulting. Charm is effortless, decisiveness is not.
     [2] The rising nakshatra colours the body and first impression: brilliant, architectural vision; polishes form into beauty.
     [3] The Lagna lord Venus sits in the 2nd house in Scorpio — Friend's Sign (Mitra), combust, composite strength 30/100 (Weak). The life's centre of gravity therefore leans toward wealth: that agenda colours everything this chart attempts, and the native's sense of self is bound up in it.
     [4] Venus occupies Jyeshtha pada 4 (lord Mercury) — eldest-born intensity; protective authority won through trials. Its nakshatra lord Mercury is a friend, so this quality comes through warmly and legibly.
     [5] Saturn (4th+5th) is the yogakaraka — patient, structural rise. Mercury as 9th lord carries fortune. Jupiter (3rd+6th) is the chief functional malefic; Mars as 2nd+7th lord is the working maraka.
  == Chandra — the mind and its weather
     [1] The Moon is in Aquarius in the 5th house — Friend's Sign (Mitra), composite strength 47/100 (Moderate). The mind is detached and observational, feeling at one remove and more comfortable with humanity than with individuals. Emotional independence is real, occasionally to the point of remoteness.
     [2] Its nakshatra is Shatabhisha pada 3 (lord Rahu) — veiled healing; solitary depths, mysticism and medicine. This is the single most personal signature in the chart — it sets the Vimshottari dasha sequence and describes how the native actually experiences their own life from the inside. Its nakshatra lord Rahu is neutral, transmitting this quality plainly and without colour.
     [3] Placed in the 5th, the emotional life orbits intelligence; that is where this native goes for comfort and where they are most easily unsettled.
  == Surya — the will and the self
     [1] The Sun is in Sagittarius in the 3rd house — Neutral Sign (Sama), composite strength 43/100 (Weak). The will is principled and expansive: identity rests on beliefs held, defended and taught.
     [2] In the 3rd house, that identity is staked on courage — this is the arena in which the native needs to matter, and failure here is felt as a failure of self rather than of circumstance.
     [3] The Sun occupies Mula pada 2 (lord Ketu) — root-cutting inquiry; destroys to find foundational truth. Its nakshatra lord Ketu is neutral, transmitting this quality plainly and without colour.
  == The chart's strong and weak axis
     [1] Moon is the strongest graha in this chart at 47/100 (Moderate), placed in the 5th house in Aquarius. Its qualities are the ones this native reaches for by default and performs best under pressure — Moon's significations are where they are genuinely, reliably competent. Its top contributions are Dignity: friend; House placement (5); Aspect from Jupiter.
     [2] Jupiter is the weakest at 24/100 (Afflicted), in the 9th house in Gemini. This is the chart's soft flank: Jupiter's significations are where the native over-compensates, avoids, or quietly outsources to other people. Its heaviest drags are Dignity: greatEnemy; Aspect from Sun; Aspect from Mercury.
     [3] The personality is largely the negotiation between these two. Growth in this chart looks less like adding to Moon — which needs no help — and more like refusing to let Jupiter's weakness set the terms, since it is precisely the area the native is most tempted to route around.
  == Atmakaraka — what this life is for
     [1] Venus is the Atmakaraka (the soul significator — the planet that travelled furthest through its sign, at 29°32'). In simple terms: whatever Venus stands for — placed in your 2nd house in Scorpio — is the lesson this life keeps returning to, in relationships, in work, everywhere. It is less "what you do" and more "what you cannot avoid becoming good at".
     [2] Its Karakamsa (the Navamsa sign of the Atmakaraka) is Pisces: the inner room where that lesson is actually worked out. A Pisces Karakamsa colours the soul's work: impressionable, compassionate and porous to atmosphere, absorbing whatever surrounds it and needing boundaries it is reluctant to build. Imagination and faith are the standing strengths.
  == Navamsa — the inner person
     [1] The Navamsa (D-9) Lagna is Libra. If the birth chart is how life presents itself, the Navamsa is how you are on the inside once the noise settles — and a Libra inner nature is gracious, balancing and relationship-oriented; this native thinks by consulting. Charm is effortless, decisiveness is not. When the outer (Libra) and inner (Libra) signs differ in element, people who only know you casually often misread you; those close to you meet the Navamsa.
     [2] Moon, Mars, Saturn are Vargottama — occupying the same sign in both D-1 and D-9. A Vargottama planet keeps its promises: what it shows on the surface is what it actually is underneath, and it holds steady under pressure.
  == Glances on the Lagna — who edits the personality
     [1] Jupiter casts its special 5th glance onto the Lagna — its fifth drishti, Jupiter's blessing on intelligence, counsel and progeny
     [2] Rahu casts its special 5th glance onto the Lagna — its fifth drishti, Rahu's amplification of intelligence and desire
     [3] Aspects on the first house edit the personality others meet: each glance above adds its colour on top of the rising sign itself.
  == How you are seen vs how you are (Arudha Lagna)
     [1] The Arudha Lagna (how the world perceives you) falls in Sagittarius, while the actual rising sign is Libra. The public image runs on Sagittarius's wavelength — expansive, principled and restless for meaning, needing both a horizon and a doctrine. Optimism is native; tact is acquired. — while the person underneath operates as Libra. The gap is not dishonesty; it is simply that reputation forms around the 3rd house's themes. Knowing which conversations are about the image and which are about you is a lifelong advantage.
  == Yoga signatures in the personality
     [1] 2 combinations stamp themselves directly on the character: Budhaditya Yoga, Chandra-Mangala Yoga.
     [2] Budhaditya Yoga — Sun and Mercury conjoin in the 3rd house: sharp administrative intellect and articulate authority, though Mercury's combustion demands the native learn to separate ego from analysis.
     [3] Chandra-Mangala Yoga — Moon and Mars conjoin — an earning combination: entrepreneurial drive fused with instinct. Wealth through self-effort, with a temper that needs channels, not suppression.
  == Functional weather — who works for this Lagna
     [1] Saturn is the yogakaraka for Libra Lagna — Saturn in the 8th, Neutral Sign (Sama), 30/100 (Weak). Its condition is the single best predictor of how far this personality converts into standing in the world, because it rules a kendra and a trikona at once.
     [2] Working for this Lagna: Saturn in the 8th, Neutral Sign (Sama), 30/100 (Weak); Mercury in the 3rd, Enemy Sign (Shatru), 24/100 (Afflicted); Venus in the 2nd, Friend's Sign (Mitra), 30/100 (Weak). The traits these planets govern are the ones the native can lean on without second-guessing, and their dashas feel like the personality working as designed.
     [3] Working against it: Jupiter in the 9th, Great Enemy's Sign (Adhi Shatru), 24/100 (Afflicted); Sun in the 3rd, Neutral Sign (Sama), 43/100 (Weak); Mars in the 5th, Friend's Sign (Mitra), 47/100 (Moderate). These are not evil planets — they are the ones whose natural instincts pull against this particular rising sign's interests. Where they are strong, the native has a talent that repeatedly gets them into trouble; where weak, a blind spot they rarely notice.
     [4] Neutral: Moon in the 5th, Friend's Sign (Mitra), 47/100 (Moderate) — these take their colour from whatever they associate with rather than carrying an agenda of their own.

--- houses ---
  == 1st House — Libra  occupied=false lord=Ve planets=[] aspects=[Ju:5,Ra:5]
     [1] The 1st house falls in Libra and rules self, body, vitality, temperament and the overall direction of life. No graha occupies it, so the house is judged — as the classics prescribe — by the condition of its lord and by the drishti it receives.
     [2] The 1st is ruled by Venus, placed in the 2nd house at 29°32' Scorpio — Friend's Sign (Mitra), combust. Counted from the 1st itself, Venus stands in the 2nd, a working but unremarkable station from its own house. Its placement carries the 1st's agenda into wealth — that is where these significations actually play out. Composite strength is only 30/100 (Weak); the 1st is under-resourced at the lord level, and its affairs need conscious support before they will hold weight.
     [3] The lord Venus lies in Jyeshtha pada 4, ruled by Mercury — eldest-born intensity; protective authority won through trials. Its nakshatra lord Mercury is a natural friend of Venus, so these results are passed on willingly and arrive in recognisable form.
     [4] Jupiter aspects this house from the 9th in Gemini (great enemy's sign, retrograde) — a natural benefic and a functional malefic for this Lagna. The glance is its fifth drishti, Jupiter's blessing on intelligence, counsel and progeny. Jupiter's drishti on the Lagna is among the finest protections in a chart: an optimistic, ethical temperament, a well-made body, and a habit of landing on one's feet. Weight, literal and metaphorical, is the only excess. The benefic intent is present but under-resourced: the protection is real yet thin, and arrives later than the native expects. Note the split, though: naturally benefic but functionally adverse for this Lagna, so its blessing here arrives with strings attached.
     [5] Rahu aspects this house from the 9th in Gemini (neutral sign, retrograde) — a natural malefic and functionally neutral for this Lagna. The glance is its fifth drishti, Rahu's amplification of intelligence and desire. Rahu's drishti on the Lagna amplifies the persona and blurs its edges: unusual magnetism, an unconventional self-presentation, and an identity assembled rather than inherited. Other people's projections stick to this native easily. As a malefic of moderate standing, its aspect keeps the house honest, applying pressure the native learns to work with.
     [6] The house takes 1 benefic and 1 malefic aspect — a contested field whose results swing with whichever of these planets is running its dasha.
     [7] In sum: with no occupant to speak for it, the 1st's fortunes rest on Venus's condition in the 2nd and on the drishti above. Strengthen Venus and this house improves; nothing else reaches it directly.
  == 2nd House — Scorpio  occupied=true lord=Ma planets=[Ve] aspects=[Sa:7]
     [1] The 2nd house falls in Scorpio and rules wealth, family, speech, food and accumulated resources. Venus occupies it, so this house's affairs are lived directly rather than delegated.
     [2] The 2nd is ruled by Mars, placed in the 5th house at 15°00' Aquarius — Friend's Sign (Mitra). Counted from the 2nd itself, Mars stands in the 4th — a kendra or trikona from its own house, so the lord actively supports what it rules. Its placement carries the 2nd's agenda into intelligence — that is where these significations actually play out. Composite strength 47/100 (Moderate) — Mars can carry the 2nd, but results here track the native's effort closely and are not gifted.
     [3] The lord Mars lies in Shatabhisha pada 3, ruled by Rahu — veiled healing; solitary depths, mysticism and medicine. Its nakshatra lord Rahu is neutral toward Mars: results are transmitted faithfully, but without amplification.
     [4] Venus at 29°32' Scorpio: As lord of the 1st and 8th for Libra Lagna, Venus governs self and longevity and operates as a functional benefic for this Lagna. Venus in the 2nd sweetens wealth and speech alike: money through arts, luxury goods, finance and beauty industries. The voice may literally be musical. Family life is refined; the palate is expensive and knows it. Venus is in a friendly sign; the placement cooperates with the native rather than resisting. Venus is combust (asta), scorched within the Sun's orb: its outward significations are weakened while the ego colonises its portfolio; humility and deliberate cultivation of this planet's virtues are the remedy. Note (Bhava Chalit): by Sripati cusps this planet actually operates from the 3rd bhava — read its concrete, event-level results there, while its sign-based dignity stays as above.
     [5] Venus lies in Jyeshtha pada 4, ruled by Mercury — eldest-born intensity; protective authority won through trials. Its nakshatra lord Mercury is a natural friend of Venus, so these results are passed on willingly and arrive in recognisable form.
     [6] Saturn aspects this house from the 8th in Taurus (neutral sign, retrograde) — a natural malefic and the yogakaraka for this Lagna. The glance is its full seventh drishti, Saturn's direct weight and the one that delays. Saturn's aspect on the 2nd disciplines wealth and speech alike — accumulation is slow, deliberate and permanent; words are few and load-bearing. Early family life carries constraint, later provision is secure. As a malefic of moderate standing, its aspect keeps the house honest, applying pressure the native learns to work with. As the yogakaraka for this Lagna, its glance is among the chart's best structural supports wherever it lands.
     [7] Every drishti reaching this house is malefic. With no benefic counterweight, these significations need conscious defence, and the house tends to mature through difficulty rather than ease.
  == 3rd House — Sagittarius  occupied=true lord=Ju planets=[Su,Me,Ke] aspects=[Ju:7,Ra:7]
     [1] The 3rd house falls in Sagittarius and rules courage, siblings, communication, skills and self-effort. Its combined occupancy makes this a major theatre of the life.
     [2] The 3rd is ruled by Jupiter, placed in the 9th house at 18°14' Gemini — Great Enemy's Sign (Adhi Shatru), retrograde. Counted from the 3rd itself, Jupiter stands in the 7th — a kendra or trikona from its own house, so the lord actively supports what it rules. Its placement carries the 3rd's agenda into fortune — that is where these significations actually play out. Composite strength is only 24/100 (Afflicted); the 3rd is under-resourced at the lord level, and its affairs need conscious support before they will hold weight.
     [3] The lord Jupiter lies in Ardra pada 4, ruled by Rahu — stormy, penetrating intellect; breakthroughs after inner turbulence. Its nakshatra lord Rahu is neutral toward Jupiter: results are transmitted faithfully, but without amplification.
     [4] Sun at 5°23' Sagittarius: As lord of the 11th for Libra Lagna, Sun governs gains and operates as a functional malefic for this Lagna. Sun in the 3rd is one of its best placements by house: courage backed by follow-through. The native out-works siblings and rivals alike, writes and speaks with conviction, and takes initiatives that others only discuss. Younger siblings may live in this person's shadow. Sun stands on neutral ground — outcomes here track effort almost exactly, with neither subsidy nor surcharge.
     [5] Sun lies in Mula pada 2, ruled by Ketu — root-cutting inquiry; destroys to find foundational truth. Its nakshatra lord Ketu is neutral toward Sun: results are transmitted faithfully, but without amplification.
     [6] Mercury at 14°27' Sagittarius: As lord of the 9th and 12th for Libra Lagna, Mercury governs fortune and expenditure and operates as a functional benefic for this Lagna. Mercury in the 3rd is Mercury doubled — communication in the house of communication. Media, marketing, coding and commerce all flourish. Siblings are clever company. The mind darts; finishing what is started is the discipline to build. Mercury sits in an enemy's sign; its agenda meets friction from the landlord, and results require roughly double the usual persistence. Mercury is combust (asta), scorched within the Sun's orb: its outward significations are weakened while the ego colonises its portfolio; humility and deliberate cultivation of this planet's virtues are the remedy.
     [7] Mercury lies in Purva Ashadha pada 1, ruled by Venus — invincible early victories; proud, philosophical, water-blessed. Its nakshatra lord Venus is a natural friend of Mercury, so these results are passed on willingly and arrive in recognisable form.
     [8] Ketu at 3°03' Sagittarius: Ketu owns no sign and acts through its dispositor, nakshatra lord and conjunctions — a karmic agent rather than a functional lord. Ketu in the 3rd gives effortless, headless courage — bravery without the need for applause. Sibling bonds carry past-life residue. Technical intuition (code, machines, mantra) works below the level of explanation. Ketu sits in an enemy's sign; its agenda meets friction from the landlord, and results require roughly double the usual persistence. Ketu is retrograde (vakri): its energy internalises and intensifies, revisiting its themes repeatedly across the life — matters conclude on the second or third pass, not the first.
     [9] Ketu lies in Mula pada 1, ruled by Ketu — root-cutting inquiry; destroys to find foundational truth. Ketu occupies its own nakshatra, so its intent reaches the world undiluted — what it promises, it delivers in its own voice.
     [10] Sun and Ketu share Ketu as nakshatra lord, which compounds their conjunction: they do not merely share a sign, they answer to the same authority, and Ketu's periods bring both to the surface at once.
     [11] Jupiter aspects this house from the 9th in Gemini (great enemy's sign, retrograde) — a natural benefic and a functional malefic for this Lagna. The glance is its full seventh drishti, Jupiter's direct protection. Jupiter's glance on the 3rd raises courage into principle and communication into teaching; skills are pursued for their meaning. Siblings are a source of support rather than rivalry. The benefic intent is present but under-resourced: the protection is real yet thin, and arrives later than the native expects. Note the split, though: naturally benefic but functionally adverse for this Lagna, so its blessing here arrives with strings attached.
     [12] Rahu aspects this house from the 9th in Gemini (neutral sign, retrograde) — a natural malefic and functionally neutral for this Lagna. The glance is its full seventh drishti, Rahu's direct and obsessive aspect. Rahu's glance on the 3rd gives outsized ambition and boldness in self-effort, talent for media, technology and mass communication, and courage bordering on recklessness. Sibling relations are complicated or distant. As a malefic of moderate standing, its aspect keeps the house honest, applying pressure the native learns to work with.
     [13] The house takes 1 benefic and 1 malefic aspect — a contested field whose results swing with whichever of these planets is running its dasha.
     [14] Sun–Mercury (Budhaditya) welds intellect to authority — administrative brilliance and articulate command. When Mercury sits deep in combustion, cleverness serves ego; with distance, it advises it. Combined force is just 28/100 across 9.1°. This combination is compromised — its difficulties will be felt more reliably than its gifts, and remedial strengthening of Sun is the highest-leverage intervention available.
     [15] Sun–Ketu quietly detaches the ego from its throne — authority exercised without appetite for it. Identity questions run deep, often father-linked. The strength here is incorruptibility: what this native never craved cannot be used to buy them. The span is 2.3° and the combined force only 50/100: these grahas share a field more than a purpose. The promise is real but muted, and tends to surface mainly during Sun's periods.
     [16] Mercury–Ketu perceives past the syntax: intuitive analysis, mathematical or mystical shortcuts, speech that ends conversations by finishing them. Detail-tracking bores it. Best in research, jyotisha, code and any field where insight outranks paperwork. Combined force is just 28/100 across 11.4°. This combination is compromised — its difficulties will be felt more reliably than its gifts, and remedial strengthening of Ketu is the highest-leverage intervention available.
     [17] With 3 grahas sharing one field, the house becomes a committee: Sun chairs it by composite strength, the group spans 11.4° and carries a combined force of 25/100 (Afflicted). The concentration is a liability rather than an asset here — the house is overloaded, and its significations need deliberate, sustained repair.
  == 4th House — Capricorn  occupied=false lord=Sa planets=[] aspects=[]
     [1] The 4th house falls in Capricorn and rules home, mother, emotional foundations, property and inner contentment. No graha occupies it, so the house is judged — as the classics prescribe — by the condition of its lord and by the drishti it receives.
     [2] The 4th is ruled by Saturn, placed in the 8th house at 16°12' Taurus — Neutral Sign (Sama), retrograde. Counted from the 4th itself, Saturn stands in the 5th — a kendra or trikona from its own house, so the lord actively supports what it rules. Its placement carries the 4th's agenda into longevity — that is where these significations actually play out. Composite strength is only 30/100 (Weak); the 4th is under-resourced at the lord level, and its affairs need conscious support before they will hold weight.
     [3] The lord Saturn lies in Rohini pada 2, ruled by Moon — magnetic, sensual growth; fertile creativity and material fascination. Its nakshatra lord Moon is a natural enemy of Saturn, so even a well-placed Saturn has its results filtered through an unsympathetic agent — expect the promise to arrive altered, later, or at a price.
     [4] No graha casts drishti on the 4th. Its affairs run on the lord's condition alone, with neither external reinforcement nor interference — outcomes here are quieter and more self-determined than elsewhere in the chart.
     [5] In sum: with no occupant to speak for it, the 4th's fortunes rest on Saturn's condition in the 8th and on the drishti above. Strengthen Saturn and this house improves; nothing else reaches it directly.
  == 5th House — Aquarius  occupied=true lord=Sa planets=[Mo,Ma] aspects=[Ju:9,Sa:10,Ra:9]
     [1] The 5th house falls in Aquarius and rules intelligence, children, creativity, romance and purva-punya (past merit). Its combined occupancy makes this a major theatre of the life.
     [2] The 5th is ruled by Saturn, placed in the 8th house at 16°12' Taurus — Neutral Sign (Sama), retrograde. Counted from the 5th itself, Saturn stands in the 4th — a kendra or trikona from its own house, so the lord actively supports what it rules. Its placement carries the 5th's agenda into longevity — that is where these significations actually play out. Composite strength is only 30/100 (Weak); the 5th is under-resourced at the lord level, and its affairs need conscious support before they will hold weight.
     [3] The lord Saturn lies in Rohini pada 2, ruled by Moon — magnetic, sensual growth; fertile creativity and material fascination. Its nakshatra lord Moon is a natural enemy of Saturn, so even a well-placed Saturn has its results filtered through an unsympathetic agent — expect the promise to arrive altered, later, or at a price.
     [4] Moon at 15°59' Aquarius: As lord of the 10th for Libra Lagna, Moon governs career and operates as functionally neutral for this Lagna. Moon in the 5th romanticises everything it touches — creativity, children, love affairs. The mind is fertile and the heart leads decisions; speculation tempts, and discipline in it must be borrowed from elsewhere in the chart. Moon is in a friendly sign; the placement cooperates with the native rather than resisting.
     [5] Moon lies in Shatabhisha pada 3, ruled by Rahu — veiled healing; solitary depths, mysticism and medicine. Its nakshatra lord Rahu is neutral toward Moon: results are transmitted faithfully, but without amplification.
     [6] Mars at 15°00' Aquarius: As lord of the 2nd and 7th for Libra Lagna, Mars governs wealth and marriage and operates as a functional malefic for this Lagna, carrying maraka responsibility as well. Mars in the 5th makes passion the co-author of intellect: sharp, decisive thinking; fierce love; sports-minded children. Romance is pursued like a campaign. Speculation is dangerous — this Mars doubles the stake when it should fold. Mars is in a friendly sign; the placement cooperates with the native rather than resisting.
     [7] Mars lies in Shatabhisha pada 3, ruled by Rahu — veiled healing; solitary depths, mysticism and medicine. Its nakshatra lord Rahu is neutral toward Mars: results are transmitted faithfully, but without amplification.
     [8] Moon and Mars share Rahu as nakshatra lord, which compounds their conjunction: they do not merely share a sign, they answer to the same authority, and Rahu's periods bring both to the surface at once.
     [9] Jupiter aspects this house from the 9th in Gemini (great enemy's sign, retrograde) — a natural benefic and a functional malefic for this Lagna. The glance is its ninth drishti, the blessing on fortune and dharma and the most benevolent aspect in the chart. Jupiter on the 5th is its own best signification — wisdom, sound counsel, children who are a blessing, and purva-punya that pays out in this life. Intelligence is broad rather than narrow, and the native teaches naturally. The benefic intent is present but under-resourced: the protection is real yet thin, and arrives later than the native expects. Note the split, though: naturally benefic but functionally adverse for this Lagna, so its blessing here arrives with strings attached.
     [10] Saturn aspects this house from the 8th in Taurus (neutral sign, retrograde) — a natural malefic and the yogakaraka for this Lagna. The glance is its tenth drishti, the aspect of imposed duty and scrutiny. Saturn's glance on the 5th delays and disciplines the 5th's promises: children come late or few, romance is serious, and intelligence is deep rather than quick. Purva-punya here is a debt repaid before it is drawn on. As a malefic of moderate standing, its aspect keeps the house honest, applying pressure the native learns to work with. As the yogakaraka for this Lagna, its glance is among the chart's best structural supports wherever it lands.
     [11] Rahu aspects this house from the 9th in Gemini (neutral sign, retrograde) — a natural malefic and functionally neutral for this Lagna. The glance is its ninth drishti, Rahu's distortion of inherited fortune and belief. Rahu's glance on the 5th makes intelligence unconventional and speculative, romance intense and rule-breaking, and matters of children a site of either delay or unusual circumstance. Speculation attracts, and must be governed. As a malefic of moderate standing, its aspect keeps the house honest, applying pressure the native learns to work with.
     [12] The house takes 1 benefic and 2 malefic aspects — a contested field whose results swing with whichever of these planets is running its dasha.
     [13] Moon–Mars (Chandra-Mangala) monetises instinct: entrepreneurial reflexes, protective passion, wealth built through decisive emotional intelligence. The same circuitry produces flash-flood tempers — profitable when dammed, destructive when not. At 1.0° the planets are genuinely blended — combined force 60/100, led by Moon. Expect the combination to express steadily rather than dramatically, in proportion to the effort put into it.

  == 7th House — Aries  occupied=false lord=Ma planets=[] aspects=[Ke:5]

Its placement carries the 7th's agenda into intelligence — that is where these significations actually play out.   == 8th House — Taurus  occupied=true lord=Ve planets=[Sa] aspects=[Ma:4,Ve:7]
     [1] The 8th house falls in Taurus and rules longevity, transformation, inheritance, occult depth and sudden events. Saturn occupies it, so this house's affairs are lived directly rather than delegated.
     [2] The 8th is ruled by Venus, placed in the 2nd house at 29°32' Scorpio — Friend's Sign (Mitra), combust. Counted from the 8th itself, Venus stands in the 7th — a kendra or trikona from its own house, so the lord actively supports what it rules. Its placement carries the 8th's agenda into wealth — that is where these significations actually play out. Composite strength is only 30/100 (Weak); the 8th is under-resourced at the lord level, and its affairs need conscious support before they will hold weight.
     [3] The lord Venus lies in Jyeshtha pada 4, ruled by Mercury — eldest-born intensity; protective authority won through trials. Its nakshatra lord Mercury is a natural friend of Venus, so these results are passed on willingly and arrive in recognisable form.
     [4] Saturn at 16°12' Taurus: As lord of the 4th and 5th for Libra Lagna, Saturn governs home and intelligence and operates as the yogakaraka for this Lagna. Saturn in the 8th confers longevity and a professional relationship with the underworld of life — taxes, legacies, chronic conditions, occult discipline. Transformations are slow, total and irreversible. This native survives what fells others. Saturn stands on neutral ground — outcomes here track effort almost exactly, with neither subsidy nor surcharge. Saturn is retrograde (vakri): its energy internalises and intensifies, revisiting its themes repeatedly across the life — matters conclude on the second or third pass, not the first.
     [5] Saturn lies in Rohini pada 2, ruled by Moon — magnetic, sensual growth; fertile creativity and material fascination. Its nakshatra lord Moon is a natural enemy of Saturn, so even a well-placed Saturn has its results filtered through an unsympathetic agent — expect the promise to arrive altered, later, or at a price.
     [6] Mars aspects this house from the 5th in Aquarius (friendly sign) — a natural malefic and a functional malefic for this Lagna. The glance is its fourth drishti, Mars at close range and the most disruptive of its three aspects. Mars's glance on the 8th gives surgical capacity, courage in crisis and interest in what lies beneath — but it also makes transformations abrupt. Accidents, surgery and sudden reversals feature; longevity holds once the native stops treating risk as recreation. As a malefic of moderate standing, its aspect keeps the house honest, applying pressure the native learns to work with.
     [7] Venus aspects this house from the 2nd in Scorpio (friendly sign, combust) — a natural benefic and a functional benefic for this Lagna. The glance is its full seventh drishti. Venus's aspect on the 8th brings gain through the partner's or others' resources, an interest in the esoteric side of love, and longevity supported by an appetite for living. Secret attachments are a recurring theme. As a benefic of moderate standing, its aspect steadies the house without transforming it.
     [8] The house takes 1 benefic and 1 malefic aspect — a contested field whose results swing with whichever of these planets is running its dasha.
  == 9th House — Gemini  occupied=true lord=Me planets=[Ju,Ra] aspects=[Su:7,Me:7,Ke:7]
     [1] The 9th house falls in Gemini and rules fortune, dharma, higher learning, the guru, father and long journeys. Its combined occupancy makes this a major theatre of the life.
     [2] The 9th is ruled by Mercury, placed in the 3rd house at 14°27' Sagittarius — Enemy Sign (Shatru), combust. Counted from the 9th itself, Mercury stands in the 7th — a kendra or trikona from its own house, so the lord actively supports what it rules. Its placement carries the 9th's agenda into courage — that is where these significations actually play out. Composite strength is only 24/100 (Afflicted); the 9th is under-resourced at the lord level, and its affairs need conscious support before they will hold weight.
     [3] The lord Mercury lies in Purva Ashadha pada 1, ruled by Venus — invincible early victories; proud, philosophical, water-blessed. Its nakshatra lord Venus is a natural friend of Mercury, so these results are passed on willingly and arrive in recognisable form.
     [4] Jupiter at 18°14' Gemini: As lord of the 3rd and 6th for Libra Lagna, Jupiter governs courage and health and operates as a functional malefic for this Lagna. Jupiter in the 9th is the guru on the guru's throne: faith, fortune, a benevolent father, and luck that looks unearned to onlookers. Higher learning, pilgrimage and publishing prosper. This native's beliefs shape their entire material outcome. Jupiter is lodged with a great enemy — expect its significations to be taxed; remedial strengthening of this planet earns outsized returns. Jupiter is retrograde (vakri): its energy internalises and intensifies, revisiting its themes repeatedly across the life — matters conclude on the second or third pass, not the first.
     [5] Jupiter lies in Ardra pada 4, ruled by Rahu — stormy, penetrating intellect; breakthroughs after inner turbulence. Its nakshatra lord Rahu is neutral toward Jupiter: results are transmitted faithfully, but without amplification.
     [6] Rahu at 3°03' Gemini: Rahu owns no sign and acts through its dispositor, nakshatra lord and conjunctions — a karmic agent rather than a functional lord. Rahu in the 9th manufactures its own dharma: foreign gurus, unorthodox philosophy, fortune through disruption of tradition. The father-relationship carries karmic static. Pilgrimage sites this native visits tend to be unlisted. Rahu stands on neutral ground — outcomes here track effort almost exactly, with neither subsidy nor surcharge. Rahu is retrograde (vakri): its energy internalises and intensifies, revisiting its themes repeatedly across the life — matters conclude on the second or third pass, not the first.
     [7] Rahu lies in Mrigashira pada 3, ruled by Mars — restless, searching mind; gentle curiosity that seeks the finer trail. Its nakshatra lord Mars is a natural enemy of Rahu, so even a well-placed Rahu has its results filtered through an unsympathetic agent — expect the promise to arrive altered, later, or at a price.
     [8] Sun aspects this house from the 3rd in Sagittarius (neutral sign) — a natural malefic and a functional malefic for this Lagna. The glance is its full seventh drishti. Sunlight on the 9th strengthens dharma, fortune and the father-signification, granting a natural relationship to teachers, doctrine and purposeful long journeys. Conviction is the strength; certainty is the risk. As a malefic of moderate standing, its aspect keeps the house honest, applying pressure the native learns to work with.
     [9] Mercury aspects this house from the 3rd in Sagittarius (enemy sign, combust) — a natural malefic and a functional benefic for this Lagna. The glance is its full seventh drishti. Mercury's aspect on the 9th makes higher learning systematic and doctrine something to be examined rather than swallowed; the native studies the tradition and then questions it. Teaching, publishing and scholarly travel are favoured. A weak malefic aspect afflicts without conferring much capability: friction here comes with little compensating discipline. Its functional role softens this considerably — a natural malefic that works for this Lagna behaves far better than its reputation.
     [10] Ketu aspects this house from the 3rd in Sagittarius (enemy sign, retrograde) — a natural malefic and functionally neutral for this Lagna. The glance is its full seventh drishti, Ketu's direct detachment. Ketu's aspect on the 9th detaches the native from inherited religion while deepening the actual practice — the form is discarded, the substance kept. The father or guru may be absent, renunciate, or simply outgrown. A weak malefic aspect afflicts without conferring much capability: friction here comes with little compensating discipline.
     [11] Every drishti reaching this house is malefic. With no benefic counterweight, these significations need conscious defence, and the house tends to mature through difficulty rather than ease.
     [12] Jupiter–Rahu (Guru-Chandala) stress-tests belief: unorthodox philosophy, magnified fortune-hunger, teachers who disappoint or liberate. At its best, genuinely original wisdom outside every syllabus; at its worst, principle rented out to ambition. Combined force is just 18/100 across 15.2°. This combination is compromised — its difficulties will be felt more reliably than its gifts, and remedial strengthening of Rahu is the highest-leverage intervention available.
  == 10th House — Cancer  occupied=false lord=Mo planets=[] aspects=[Sa:3]
     [1] The 10th house falls in Cancer and rules career, status, authority, karma and public achievement. No graha occupies it, so the house is judged — as the classics prescribe — by the condition of its lord and by the drishti it receives.

Composite strength 47/100 (Moderate) — Moon can carry the 10th, but results here track the native's effort closely and are not gifted.
     [3] The lord Moon lies in Shatabhisha pada 3, ruled by Rahu — veiled healing; solitary depths, mysticism and medicine. Its nakshatra lord Rahu is neutral toward Moon: results are transmitted faithfully, but without amplification.
     [4] Saturn aspects this house from the 8th in Taurus (neutral sign, retrograde) — a natural malefic and the yogakaraka for this Lagna. The glance is its third drishti, Saturn's grinding aspect, which wears down rather than blocks outright. Saturn on the 10th carries its directional strength by signification, and is one of the strongest career glances in the chart: authority through service, seniority through endurance, and a reputation for reliability that compounds. Advancement is slow and essentially irreversible. As a malefic of moderate standing, its aspect keeps the house honest, applying pressure the native learns to work with. As the yogakaraka for this Lagna, its glance is among the chart's best structural supports wherever it lands.
     [5] Every drishti reaching this house is malefic. With no benefic counterweight, these significations need conscious defence, and the house tends to mature through difficulty rather than ease.
     [6] In sum: with no occupant to speak for it, the 10th's fortunes rest on Moon's condition in the 5th and on the drishti above. Strengthen Moon and this house improves; nothing else reaches it directly.
  == 11th House — Leo  occupied=false lord=Su planets=[] aspects=[Mo:7,Ma:7,Ke:9]
     [1] The 11th house falls in Leo and rules gains, income, networks, elder siblings and fulfilment of desires. No graha occupies it, so the house is judged — as the classics prescribe — by the condition of its lord and by the drishti it receives.
     [2] The 11th is ruled by Sun, placed in the 3rd house at 5°23' Sagittarius — Neutral Sign (Sama). Counted from the 11th itself, Sun stands in the 5th — a kendra or trikona from its own house, so the lord actively supports what it rules. Its placement carries the 11th's agenda into courage — that is where these significations actually play out. Composite strength is only 43/100 (Weak); the 11th is under-resourced at the lord level, and its affairs need conscious support before they will hold weight.
     [3] The lord Sun lies in Mula pada 2, ruled by Ketu — root-cutting inquiry; destroys to find foundational truth. Its nakshatra lord Ketu is neutral toward Sun: results are transmitted faithfully, but without amplification.
     [4] Moon aspects this house from the 5th in Aquarius (friendly sign) — a natural benefic and functionally neutral for this Lagna. The glance is its full seventh drishti. The Moon's aspect on the 11th builds income through networks that are personal before they are professional, and gains that ebb and flow with the native's circle. Desires are many and sincerely felt. As a benefic of moderate standing, its aspect steadies the house without transforming it.
     [5] Mars aspects this house from the 5th in Aquarius (friendly sign) — a natural malefic and a functional malefic for this Lagna. The glance is its full seventh drishti, direct and unavoidable. Mars's aspect on the 11th makes gains the product of campaigns and networks the product of alliances. Income responds to aggression; elder siblings are strong presences — allies or rivals, sometimes both. As a malefic of moderate standing, its aspect keeps the house honest, applying pressure the native learns to work with.
     [6] Ketu aspects this house from the 3rd in Sagittarius (enemy sign, retrograde) — a natural malefic and functionally neutral for this Lagna. The glance is its ninth drishti, Ketu's refinement of dharma and fortune. Ketu's aspect on the 11th thins the network to a few real connections and makes gains erratic — money arrives when needed rather than when pursued. Desires themselves lose their grip, which the native may mistake for failure. A weak malefic aspect afflicts without conferring much capability: friction here comes with little compensating discipline.
     [7] The house takes 1 benefic and 2 malefic aspects — a contested field whose results swing with whichever of these planets is running its dasha.
     [8] In sum: with no occupant to speak for it, the 11th's fortunes rest on Sun's condition in the 3rd and on the drishti above. Strengthen Sun and this house improves; nothing else reaches it directly.

     [4] Mars aspects this house from the 5th in Aquarius (friendly sign) — a natural malefic and a functional malefic for this Lagna. --- section: marriage ---
  headline: Your marriage indications are mixed: warm significators on one side, some classical delay markers on the other. For a chart like this the windows matter far more than the average does.
  score=55 verdict=none confidence=75
  == The marriage houses and significators
     [1] Marriage is read from your 7th house and from the planets that naturally stand for partnership. Below are the two deeper views the tradition adds to that: the marriage pada, which describes the institution, and the Navamsa, which describes the daily life inside it.
     [2] Your Upapada Lagna (the marriage pada — the arudha of the 12th house) falls in Sagittarius, and Jupiter rules it, sitting in your 9th house, Great Enemy's Sign (Adhi Shatru). This point describes marriage as a standing institution in your life rather than as romance — the household, the in-laws, the public fact of being married. Its condition is the best single indicator of how settled and respected that arrangement tends to feel from the inside.
     [3] Your Navamsa (the D-9 divisional chart of marriage and inner strength) is where married life is actually read, and it says this: the Navamsa rising sign is Libra, ruled by Venus; its 7th house falls in Aries, with nothing in it; and Venus lands in Pisces. Treat this as the private texture of the marriage — what the two of you are like at home on an ordinary Tuesday, rather than what the relationship looks like to everyone else.
     why(0) Your 7th house is ruled by Mars, and it sits in your 5th house, Friend's Sign (Mitra), scoring 47/100 for strength. That is where your partnership life gets carried out — 5th-house matters and your marriage tend to move together.
     why(5) Venus is strong in your Navamsa, the chart that describes married life from the inside. Affection in your marriage tends to deepen with the years instead of wearing thin — the tenderness is still there after the novelty has gone. [BPHS, Ch.6 (Navamsa)]
     why(2) Sun is your Darakaraka (the spouse significator — least advanced by degree), and it sits in your 3rd house. The person you marry tends to carry that planet's signature — its temperament, its pace, its way of handling difficulty — closely enough that the description usually feels familiar once you meet them. [Jaimini Upadesa Sutras, chara karakas]
     why(-4) Venus is your natural significator for marriage, and it stands Friend's Sign (Mitra) in your 2nd house, too close to the Sun to shine on its own (30/100). A significator under pressure usually shows up as a slower, more deliberate route into partnership — you tend to learn about love the long way, which is not the same as being denied it. [BPHS, kalatra karaka]
     why(-3) Ketu casts its aspect on your 7th house, which the classics read as a "later is better" influence on marriage. In ordinary terms: early partnerships tend to teach rather than last, and the ones you choose after about thirty hold much better. [Phaladeepika, malefic influence on the 7th]
  == Mangal Dosha
     [1] Mars sits in your 5th house, which technically forms Mangal Dosha from the Moon — but your chart cancels it: Jupiter aspects Mars — the classical benefic override. That means the heat is there without the affliction the tradition warns about. In daily life it reads as directness rather than damage: you say the difficult thing early, which is usually kinder than the alternative.
  == What your partner tends to be like
     [1] Because Mars rules your 7th house, the person you marry tends to carry that planet's nature — you are drawn to it, and you recognise it quickly. Tradition also reads a direction from this, South of your birthplace, but hold that one loosely: direction rules are the softest technique on this page.
     [2] There are no strong foreign markers on your marriage axis, which leans the reading toward a partner from a familiar cultural circle. That is a tendency in the chart, not a boundary on your life — plenty of these charts marry across every kind of distance.
     [3] Sun, as your spouse significator, colours their temperament more than anything else here. Look for a strong Sun signature in whom you actually choose — it is usually more visible in how they behave under stress than in how they present at first.
  == What tradition offers, and what actually helps
     [1] The standard literature recommends strengthening Venus in the traditional way — Friday observances, white clothing, courtesy toward your partner. The last of those is the one that carries the weight: attention paid to the relationship is what the practice is really training.

--- section: cautions ---

  == How to read this
     [1] These are tendencies, not sentences, and none of them is a verdict on your life. Classical astrology lists them for one reason: so you can steer. That is why every caution below arrives with the thing that actually helps — a caution without a counter-measure is just anxiety with a citation.
     [2] Nothing here is medical, legal or financial advice, and none of it should stand in for a professional who can look at your actual situation.

  == Where these come from
     [1] From your chart: the ruler of your rising sign (Venus), your yogakaraka (Saturn), the ruler of your Moon's nakshatra (Rahu) and your strongest supportive planet (Mercury) are the ones working in your favour. Their numbers, colours, days and directions are what this section calls yours.
     [2] From your birth date: Moolank 3 and Bhagyank 9, and a name number of 3 on the chaldean system, with a Kua number of 8 — each of which tradition assigns to a planet.
     [3] Treat all of this the way tradition actually intends it: as a set of preferences that tilt the odds slightly and cost nothing to follow, not as rules that decide outcomes. Wearing your colour to an interview is worth about as much as the confidence it gives you, which is not nothing.
